习题 6.7 质量分别为 $m _ { 1 }$ 和 $m _ { 2 }$ 的两个粒子(处于一维坐标系中)，它们之间的相互作用势 $V \left( \left| x _ { 1 } - x _ { 2 } \right| \right)$ 仅和粒子之间距离有关，因此哈密顿量为

$$
\hat {H} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m _ {1}} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m _ {2}} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}} + V (| x _ {1} - x _ {2} |).
$$

作用在两粒子波函数上的平移算符为

$$
\hat {T} (a) \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) = \psi (x _ {1} - a, x _ {2} - a).
$$

(a) 证明:平移算符可以写成

$$
\hat {T} (a) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {\mathrm {i} a}{\hbar} \hat {P}},
$$

其中 $\hat { P } = \hat { p } _ { 1 } + \hat { p } _ { 2 }$ 是系统总动量

(b) 证明:该系统的总动量守恒.

证明 (a) 由于最后要证明的平移算符 ${ \hat { T } } \left( a \right)$ 写成了 $\mathrm { e }$ 指数形式，而算符的操作一般都在乘积的形式下，因此，我们应当将推导过程用泰勒求和的形式进行展开.

$$
\hat {T} (a) \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) = \psi (x _ {1} - a, x _ {2} - a)
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \sum_ {m, n} \frac {1}{m ! n !} \frac {\partial^ {m + n}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {m} \partial x _ {1} ^ {n}} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) (- a) ^ {m} (- a) ^ {n} \\ = \sum_ {m, n} \frac {1}{m ! n !} \frac {\partial^ {m + n}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {m} \partial x _ {1} ^ {n}} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) (- a) ^ {m + n} \\ = \sum_ {m, n} \frac {1}{m ! n !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} a}{\hbar}\right) ^ {m + n} (- \mathrm {i} \hbar) ^ {m} (- \mathrm {i} \hbar) ^ {n} \frac {\partial^ {m + n}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {m} \partial x _ {2} ^ {n}} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \\ = \sum_ {m, n} \frac {1}{m ! n !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} a}{\hbar}\right) ^ {m + n} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {1}}\right) ^ {m} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {2}}\right) ^ {n} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \\ = \sum_ {m, n} \frac {1}{m ! n !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} a}{\hbar}\right) ^ {m + n} (\hat {p} _ {1}) ^ {m} (\hat {p} _ {2}) ^ {n} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \\ = \sum_ {m} \frac {1}{m !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} a}{\hbar}\right) ^ {m} (\hat {p} _ {1}) ^ {m} \sum_ {n!} \frac {1}{n !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} a}{\hbar}\right) ^ {n} (\hat {p} _ {2}) ^ {n} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \\ = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a \hat {p} _ {1} / \hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a \hat {p} _ {2} / \hbar} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}). \\ \end{array}
$$

由于 $\hat { p } _ { 1 }$ 与 $\hat { p } _ { 2 }$ 对易，所以

$$
\hat {T} (a) \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a \hat {p} _ {1} / \hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a \hat {p} _ {2} / \hbar} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a (\hat {p} _ {1} + \hat {p} _ {2}) / \hbar} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a \hat {P} / \hbar} \psi (x _ {1}, x _ {2}).
$$

(b) 首先证明哈密顿量是平移不变的，然后通过平移算符与动量的关系来得到晗密顿量与动量的关系.

将平移变换后的哈密顿量作用在测试函数 $f \left( x _ { 1 } , x _ { 2 } \right)$ 上，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {H} ^ {\prime} f (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \\ = \hat {T} ^ {\dagger} (a) \hat {H} \hat {T} (a) f (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \\ = \hat {T} (- a) \left[ - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}} + V (| x _ {1} - x _ {2} |) \right] \hat {T} (a) f (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \\ = \hat {T} (- a) \left[ - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}} + V (| x _ {1} - x _ {2} |) \right] f (x _ {1} - a, x _ {2} - a) \\ = \left\{- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {\prime 2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {\prime 2}} + V [ | (x _ {1} + a) - (x _ {2} + a) | ] \right\} f (x _ {1} - a + a, x _ {2} - a + a) \\ = \left[ - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {\prime 2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {\prime 2}} + V (| x _ {1} - x _ {2} |) \right] f (x _ {1}, x _ {2}), \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $x ^ { \prime } { } _ { 1 } = x _ { 1 } + a , x ^ { \prime } { } _ { 2 } = x _ { 2 } + a$ 偏导数形式为

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {\prime}} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {1}} \frac {\partial x _ {1}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {\prime}} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {1}} \frac {\partial (x _ {1} ^ {\prime} - a)}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {\prime}} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {1}}, \quad \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {\prime 2}} = \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}};
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {\prime}} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {2}} \frac {\partial x _ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {\prime}} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {2}} \frac {\partial (x _ {2} ^ {\prime} - a)}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {\prime}} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {2}}, \quad \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {\prime 2}} = \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}}.
$$

因此，可以得到

$$
\hat {H} ^ {\prime} f (x) = \left[ - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}} + V (| x _ {1} - x _ {2} |) \right] f (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) = \hat {H} f (x).
$$

即哈密顿量在平移变换下不变.由于哈密顿量有连续平移不变性，则在无限小平移操作下也应当保持不变，

$$
\hat {T} ^ {\dagger} (\delta) \hat {H} \hat {T} (\delta) = \hat {H},
$$

$$
\left(1 + \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} \hat {p}\right) \hat {H} \left(1 - \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} \hat {p}\right) = \hat {H},
$$

$$
\hat {H} + \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} \hat {p} \hat {H} - \hat {H} \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} \hat {p} - \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} \hat {p} \hat {H} \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} \hat {p} = \hat {H}.
$$

其中用到平移算符的泰勒展开并略去高阶小量，得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} \hat {p} \hat {H} - \hat {H} \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} \hat {p} = 0,
$$

$$
\left[ \hat {p}, \hat {H} \right] = 0.
$$

因此，根据广义埃伦菲斯特定理可知系统的动量守恒.

# *习题 6.8

(a) 证明:宇称算符 $\hat { \Pi }$ 是厄米的.   
(b) 证明:宇称算符的本征值是土 $\pm 1$

证明 (a) 从厄米算符的定义出发

$$
\left\langle f (x) \right| \hat {\Pi} g (x) \Bigg \rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} f ^ {*} (x) \hat {\Pi} g (x) d x = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} f ^ {*} (x) g (- x) d x,
$$

令 $x = - t$ 则上式为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} f ^ {*} (x) g (- x) d x = \int_ {\infty} ^ {- \infty} f ^ {*} (- t) g (t) d (- t) \\ = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left[ \hat {\Pi} f (t) \right] ^ {*} g (t) d t = \left\langle \hat {\Pi} f (t) \mid g (t) \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

结合以上两式，可得( $ f ( x ) | \hat { \Pi } g ( x )  =  \hat { \Pi } f ( x ) | g ( x ) $ 即字称算符 $\hat { \Pi }$ 是厄米的

(b) 令字称算符的本征值为 $\lambda$ ，则

$$
\hat {\Pi} f (x) = \lambda f (- x),
$$

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {2} f (x) = \hat {\Pi} \lambda f (- x) = \lambda^ {2} f (x) = f (x).
$$

所以

$$
\lambda^ {2} = 1.
$$

又因为字称算符是厄米的，所以本征值只能为实数，即

$$
\lambda = \pm 1.
$$

# 事习题 6.9

(a) "真"标量算符在字称操作下是不变的:

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {f} \hat {\Pi} = \hat {f},
$$

然而，质标量改变符号 证明:对于"真"标量有 $\left[ \hat { \Pi } , \hat { f } \right] \ = \ 0$ ，且对于厦标量$\left\{ \hat { \Pi } , \hat { f } \right\} = 0$ 注释两个算符 $\hat { A }$ 和 $\hat { B }$ 的反对易关系定义为 $\left\{ \hat { A } , \hat { B } \right\} \equiv \hat { A } \hat { B } + \hat { B } \hat { A }$

(b) 类似地，"真"矢量在宇称操作下改变符号

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {V} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {V},
$$

厦矢量则不改变符号. 证明:对于"真"矢量有( $\left\{ \hat { \Pi } , \hat { V } \right\} = { \bf 0 }$ ，且对于厦矢量有$\left[ \hat { \Pi } , \hat { V } \right] = \mathbf { 0 } .$

证明 (时对于"真"标量算符

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {f} \hat {\Pi} = \hat {f},
$$

$$
\hat {\Pi} \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {f} \hat {\Pi} = \hat {\Pi} \hat {f},
$$

$$
\hat {f} \hat {\Pi} = \hat {\Pi} \hat {f},
$$

$$
\left[ \hat {\Pi}, \hat {f} \right] = 0.
$$

对于眉标量算符

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {f} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {f},
$$

$$
\hat {\Pi} \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {f} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {\Pi} \hat {f},
$$

$$
\hat {f} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {\Pi} \hat {f},
$$

$$
\left\{\hat {\Pi}, \hat {f} \right\} = 0.
$$

对于"真"矢量算符

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {V} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {V},
$$

$$
\hat {\Pi} \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {V} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {\Pi} \hat {V},
$$

$$
\hat {V} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {\Pi} \hat {V},
$$

$$
\left\{\hat {\Pi}, \hat {V} \right\} = \mathbf {0}.
$$

对于腐矢量算符

$$
\hat {\Pi} \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {V} \hat {\Pi} = \hat {\Pi} \hat {V},
$$

$$
\hat {V} \hat {\Pi} = \hat {\Pi} \hat {V},
$$

$$
\left[ \hat {\Pi}, \hat {V} \right] = 0.
$$

习题 6.10 证明:位置和动量算符是奇宇称的.也就是证明方程 (6.18) (6.19)并将其扩展到三维情况下方程 (6.21) (6.22).

证明 由于字称算符的么正性，即

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {- 1} = \hat {\Pi} = \left(\hat {\Pi}\right) ^ {\dagger}.
$$

变换后的坐标算符作用在测试函数上

$$
\hat {x} ^ {\prime} f (x) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {x} \hat {\Pi} f (x) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {x} f (- x) = \hat {\Pi} x f (- x) = - x f (x) = - \hat {x} f (x),
$$

RP

$$
\hat {x} ^ {\prime} = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {x} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {x}. \tag {6.18}
$$

变换后的动量算符作用在测试函数上

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {p} ^ {\prime} f (x) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} \hat {\Pi} f (x) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} f (- x) \\ = \hat {\Pi} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) f (- x) = \left[ - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} (- x)} \right] f (x) \\ = - \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) f (x) = - \hat {p} f (x). \\ \end{array}
$$

即

$$
\hat {p} ^ {\prime} = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {p}. \tag {6.19}
$$

从一维推广到三维的情况:

对于坐标算符，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {x} ^ {\prime} f (x) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {x} \hat {\Pi} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {x} f (- x, - y, - z) = \hat {\Pi} x f (- x, - y, - z) \\ = - x f (x, y, z) = - \hat {x} f (x, y, z), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {y} ^ {\prime} f (x) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {y} \hat {\Pi} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {y} f (- x, - y, - z) = \hat {\Pi} y f (- x, - y, - z) \\ = - y f (x, y, z) = - \hat {y} f (x, y, z), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {z} ^ {\prime} f (x) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {z} \hat {\Pi} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {z} f (- x, - y, - z) = \hat {\Pi} z f (- x, - y, - z) \\ = - z f (x, y, z) = - \hat {z} f (x, y, z). \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\hat {\boldsymbol {r}} ^ {\prime} = \hat {\Pi} + \hat {\boldsymbol {r}} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {\boldsymbol {r}}. \tag {6.21}
$$

对于动量算符，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {p} _ {x} ^ {\prime} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} _ {x} \hat {\Pi} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} _ {x} f (- x, - y, - z) \\ = \hat {\Pi} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) f (- x, - y, - z) = \left[ - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} (- x)} \right] f (x, y, z) \\ = - \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) f (x, y, z) = - \hat {p} _ {x} f (x, y, z), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {p} _ {y} ^ {\prime} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} _ {y} \hat {\Pi} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} _ {y} f (- x, - y, - z) \\ = \hat {\Pi} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} y}\right) f (- x, - y, - z) = \left[ - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} (- y)} \right] f (x, y, z) \\ = - \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} y}\right) f (x, y, z) = - \hat {p} _ {y} f (x, y, z), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {p} _ {z} ^ {\prime} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} _ {z} \hat {\Pi} f (x, y, z) = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} _ {z} f (- x, - y, - z) \\ = \hat {\Pi} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} z}\right) f (- x, - y, - z) = \left[ - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} (- z)} \right] f (x, y, z) \\ = - \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} z}\right) f (x, y, z) = - \hat {p} _ {z} f (x, y, z). \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\hat {\boldsymbol {p}} ^ {\prime} = \hat {\Pi} + \hat {\boldsymbol {p}} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {\boldsymbol {p}}. \tag {6.22}
$$

*习题 6.11 考虑 $\hat { L }$ 处于两个确定宇称态之间的矩阵元: $\langle n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } | \hat { \pmb { L } } | n \ell m \rangle$ . 在什么情况下矩阵元会消失?注释 同样的选择定则可以用于膺矢量算符或者任意"真"标量算符.

解答由习题 6.10

$$
\hat {\pmb {r} ^ {\prime}} = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {\pmb {r}} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {\pmb {r}} \text {和} \hat {\pmb {p} ^ {\prime}} = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {\pmb {r}} \hat {\Pi} = - \hat {\pmb {p}}.
$$

因此， ${ \hat { \Pi } } ^ { \dagger } { \hat { \cal L } } { \hat { \Pi } } = { \hat { \cal L } } ^ { \prime } = { \hat { r } } ^ { \prime } \times { \hat { p } } ^ { \prime } = ( - { \hat { r } } ) \times ( - { \hat { p } } ) = { \hat { r } } \times { \hat { p } } = { \hat { L } }$ 所以，

$$
\left\langle n ^ {\prime} l ^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {L} \hat {\Pi} | n l m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} l ^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} | n l m \rangle .
$$

又由习题 6.1(c) 的结论 $\hat { \Pi } \psi _ { n \ell m } \left( r , \theta , \phi \right) = \left( - 1 \right) ^ { \ell } \psi _ { n \ell m } \left( r , \theta , \phi \right)$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \left| \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} \hat {\Pi} \right| n \ell m \right\rangle = \left\langle \hat {\Pi} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} | \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} | \hat {\Pi} n \ell m \right\rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \left| (- 1) ^ {\ell^ {\prime}} \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} (- 1) ^ {\ell} \right| n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \left| (- 1) ^ {\ell^ {\prime} + t} \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} \right| n \ell m \right\rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} | \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} | n \ell m \right\rangle . \\ \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ (- 1) ^ {\ell^ {\prime} + \ell} - 1 \right] \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} | n \ell m \rangle = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

当两个态的字称相同时， $( - 1 ) ^ { \ell ^ { \prime } + \ell } - 1 = 0$ 确保上式成立.

习题 6.12 像轨道角动量 $\hat { L }$ 一样，自旋角动量 $\hat { S }$ 具有偶宇称:

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {S} \hat {\Pi} = \hat {S}   \text {或}     \left[ \hat {\Pi},   \hat {S} \right] = 0. \tag {6.27}
$$

宇称算符作用在标准基下的旋量上(方程 (4.139))，将变成一个 $2 \times 2$ 的矩阵.证明:根据方程 (6.27)，该矩阵必须是单位矩阵的常数倍.这样一来，由于具有相同本征值的两个自旋态都是宇称的本征态，旋量的宇称就没有意义.可以任意将宇称选择为$+ 1$ ，因此宇称算符对波函数的自旋部分没有影响.①

证明设字称算符为三阶方阵

$$
\hat {\Pi} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a & b \\ d & c \end{array} \right].
$$

由宇称算符的么正性 $\hat { \Pi } = \hat { \Pi } ^ { \dagger } = \hat { \Pi } ^ { - }$ ，得

$$
\hat {\Pi} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a & b \\ d & c \end{array} \right] = \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a ^ {*} & d ^ {*} \\ b ^ {*} & c ^ {*} \end{array} \right].
$$

所以， $a = a ^ { * } , b = d ^ { * } , c = c ^ { * }$ 气因此，

$$
\hat {\Pi} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a & b \\ b ^ {*} & c \end{array} \right],
$$

其中 $a , \ b , \ c$ $c$ 都为实数.

$S _ { x } = { \frac { \hbar } { 2 } } \left[ { \begin{array} { l l } { 0 } & { 1 } \\ { 1 } & { 0 } \end{array} } \right]$ $S _ { y } = \frac { \hbar } { 2 } \left[ \begin{array} { c c } { 0 } & { - \mathrm { i } } \\ { \mathrm { i } } & { 0 } \end{array} \right] ,$

$$
\left[ \hat {\Pi}, S _ {x} \right] = \hat {\Pi} \boldsymbol {S} _ {x} - \boldsymbol {S} _ {x} \hat {\Pi} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a & b \\ b ^ {*} & c \end{array} \right] \frac {\hbar}{2} \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right] - \frac {\hbar}{2} \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a & b \\ b ^ {*} & c \end{array} \right]
$$

$$
= \frac {\hbar}{2} \left[ \begin{array}{c c} b - b ^ {*} & a - c \\ - a + c & - b + b ^ {*} \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 \end{array} \right].
$$

$b = b ^ { * } , a = c$ 以直 ${ \hat { \Pi } } = { \left[ \begin{array} { l l } { a } & { b } \\ { b } & { a } \end{array} \right] }$

再由

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {\Pi}, S _ {y} \right] = \hat {\Pi} \boldsymbol {S} _ {y} - \boldsymbol {S} _ {y} \hat {\Pi} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a & b \\ b & a \end{array} \right] \frac {\hbar}{2} \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 0 & - \mathrm {i} \\ \mathrm {i} & 0 \end{array} \right] - \frac {\hbar}{2} \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 0 & - \mathrm {i} \\ \mathrm {i} & 0 \end{array} \right] \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a & b \\ b & a \end{array} \right] \\ = \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{2} \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 2 b & 0 \\ 0 & - 2 b \end{array} \right] = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 \end{array} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此 $b = 0$ .

这样，

$$
\hat {\Pi} = \left[ \begin{array}{c c} a & 0 \\ 0 & a \end{array} \right] = a \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right] = a I.
$$

$S _ { z } = \frac { \hbar } { 2 } \left[ \begin{array} { c c } { 1 } & { 0 } \\ { 0 } & { - 1 } \end{array} \right]$ 阳也对易

事习题 6.13 考虑氢原子中的电子

(a) 证明:如果电子处于基态，那么必须有 $\langle p _ { \mathrm { e } } \rangle = 0$ . 不允许计算.  
(b) 证明:如果电子处于 $n = 2$ 态，那么( $\langle p _ { \mathrm { e } } \rangle$ 不必须为零.给出处于 $n = 2$ 能级且使得 $\langle p _ { \mathrm { e } } \rangle$ 不为军的波函数的例子，计算该态下的( $\langle p _ { \mathrm { e } } \rangle$ .

证明 (a) $p _ { \mathrm { e } } = q \hat { r }$ 字称算符作用下

$$
\hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} p _ {e} \hat {\Pi} = q \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \hat {r} \hat {\Pi} = - q \hat {r} = - p _ {e}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} \rangle = - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \left| \hat {\Pi} ^ {\dagger} \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} \hat {\Pi} \right| n \ell m \right\rangle = - \left\langle \hat {\Pi} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | \hat {\Pi} n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \left| (- 1) ^ {c ^ {\prime}} \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} (- 1) ^ {\prime} \right| n \ell m \right\rangle = (- 1) ^ {1 + l + l ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \left| \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} \right| n \ell m \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

处于基态时，上式为( $\langle { p _ { \mathrm { e } } } \rangle = - \left. { n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } } | { p _ { \mathrm { e } } } | n \ell m \right.$ 因此 $\langle p _ { \mathrm { e } } \rangle = 0$ .

电子处于 $n = 2$ 的态，只要 $\ell + \ell ^ { \prime }$ 为奇数，则( $\left. \pmb { p } _ { e } \right.$ 不必须为零，这就要求波函数为本征态的结性组合，困为单一的本征态必导致( $\ell + \ell ^ { \prime }$ 为偶数.

满足归一化条件的波函数为 $\frac { \langle 2 0 0 | + \langle 2 1 0 | } { \sqrt { 2 } }$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} \rangle = \left(\frac {\langle 2 0 0 | + \langle 2 1 0 |}{\sqrt {2}}\right) \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} \left(\frac {| 2 0 0 \rangle + | 2 1 0 \rangle}{\sqrt {2}}\right) \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left(\langle 2 0 0 | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 0 0 \rangle + 2 \operatorname {R e} \left[ \langle 2 1 0 | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 0 0 \rangle \right] + \langle 2 1 0 | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 1 0 \rangle\right) \\ = \operatorname {R e} [ \langle 2 1 0 | p _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 0 0 \rangle ] \\ = - e \operatorname {R e} \left[ \int \psi_ {2 1 0} ^ {*} (r) r \sin \theta \cos \phi \psi_ {2 0 0} (r) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r \hat {i} + \int \psi_ {2 1 0} ^ {*} (r) r \sin \theta \sin \phi \psi_ {2 0 0} (r) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r \hat {j} \right. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. + \int \psi_ {2 1 0} ^ {*} (r) r \cos \theta \psi_ {2 0 0} (r) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r \hat {k} \right] \\ = 3 e a \hat {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

串串习题 6.14 本题将建立方程 (6.30) (6.31) 之间的联系.

(a) 对角化矩附

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & - \varphi / N \\ \varphi / N & 1 \end{array} \right)
$$

得到矩阵

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} ^ {\prime} = \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} ^ {- 1},
$$

其中 $s ^ { - 1 }$ 为去正矩阵，它的列为 $\mathbf { \mathcal { M } }$ (归一化)的本征矢.

(b) 用二项式展开证明! $\operatorname* { l i m } _ { N \to \infty } \left( \mathcal { M } ^ { \prime } \right) ^ { N }$ 是一个以 $\mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } \varphi }$ 和 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } \varphi }$ 为对角元素的对角矩阵.  
(c) 变换到初始的基矢，证明:

$$
\lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} ^ {N} = \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} ^ {- 1} \left[ \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \left(\boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {N} \right] \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}}
$$

与方程 (6.31) 中的矩阵一致.

$\psi = { \left( \begin{array} { l } { a } \\ { b } \end{array} \right) }$ )，本阳 $\lambda$

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} _ {\psi} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & - \varphi / N \\ \varphi / N & 1 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} a \\ b \end{array} \right) = \lambda \left( \begin{array}{c} a \\ b \end{array} \right).
$$

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 - \lambda & - \varphi / N \\ \varphi / N & 1 - \lambda \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} a \\ b \end{array} \right) = 0.
$$

解久期方程的本征值，

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} 1 - \lambda & - \varphi / N \\ \varphi / N & 1 - \lambda \end{array} \right| = (1 - \lambda) ^ {2} + (\varphi / N) ^ {2} = 0,
$$

得 $\lambda = 1 \pm \mathrm { i } \varphi / N$ 将本征值代入

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 - \lambda & - \varphi / N \\ \varphi / N & 1 - \lambda \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} a \\ b \end{array} \right) = 0,
$$

得

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} \mp \mathrm {i} \varphi / N & - \varphi / N \\ \varphi / N & \mp \mathrm {i} \varphi / N \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} a \\ b \end{array} \right) = 0,
$$

对应本征值 $\lambda = 1 \pm \mathrm { i } \varphi / N$ 的归一化本征矢为

$$
\frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ - \mathrm {i} \end{array} \right), \quad \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ \mathrm {i} \end{array} \right).
$$

因此，可以构造

$$
\pmb {S} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c} {{1}} & {{1}} \\ {{- \mathrm {i}}} & {{\mathrm {i}}} \end{array} \right)   \text {和}   \pmb {S} ^ {- 1} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c} {{1}} & {{\mathrm {i}}} \\ {{1}} & {{- \mathrm {i}}} \end{array} \right),
$$

其中 $s$ 为么正矩阵.

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {M} ^ {\prime} = \mathcal {S} ^ {- 1} \mathcal {M} \mathcal {S} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & \mathrm {i} \\ 1 & - \mathrm {i} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & - \varphi / N \\ \varphi / N & 1 \end{array} \right) \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ - \mathrm {i} & \mathrm {i} \end{array} \right) \\ = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 + \mathrm {i} \varphi / N & 0 \\ 0 & 1 - \mathrm {i} \varphi / N \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

(b)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lim  _ {N \to \infty} \left(\mathcal {M} ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {N} = \lim  _ {N \to \infty} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 + \mathrm {i} \varphi / N & 0 \\ 0 & 1 - \mathrm {i} \varphi / N \end{array} \right) ^ {N} \\ = \lim  _ {N \to \infty} \left( \begin{array}{c c} (1 + \mathrm {i} \varphi / N) ^ {N} & 0 \\ 0 & (1 - \mathrm {i} \varphi / N) ^ {N} \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} (1 \pm \mathrm {i} \varphi / N) ^ {N} = \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {N} \mathrm {C} _ {N} ^ {k} (\pm \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{N}) ^ {k} = \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {N} \frac {N !}{(N - k) ! k !} (\pm \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{N}) ^ {k} \\ = \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {N} \frac {N !}{(N - k) ! N ^ {k}} \frac {\left(\pm \mathrm {i} \varphi\right) ^ {k}}{k !} = \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {N} \frac {N !}{(N - k) ! N ^ {k}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \varphi} \\ = \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {N} \overbrace {\frac {N (N - 1) \cdots (N - k + 1)}{N ^ {k}}} ^ {k} \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \varphi} \\ = \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \prod_ {m = 0} ^ {k - 1} \left(\frac {N - m}{N}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \varphi} \\ = \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \prod_ {m = 0} ^ {k - 1} \left(1 - \frac {m}{N}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \varphi} \\ = \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \varphi}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\lim  _ {N \to \infty} \left(\boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {N} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \varphi} & 0 \\ 0 & \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \varphi} \end{array} \right).
$$

(c)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} (\mathcal {M}) ^ {N} = \mathcal {S} \lim  _ {N \rightarrow \infty} \left(\mathcal {M} ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {N} \mathcal {S} ^ {-} \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ - \mathrm {i} & \mathrm {i} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \varphi} & 0 \\ 0 & \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \varphi} \end{array} \right) \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & \mathrm {i} \\ 1 & - \mathrm {i} \end{array} \right) \\ = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \varphi & - \sin \varphi \\ \sin \varphi & \cos \varphi \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

证明:方程 (6.34) 如何确保标量算符旋转不变串习题 6.15

$$
\hat {f} ^ {\prime} = \hat {R} ^ {\dagger} \hat {f} \hat {R} = \hat {f}.
$$

证明 $\mathbf { \nabla } _ { \mathbf { \eta } _ { n } }$ 为主轴，旋转一个无限小的 角度?则旋转操作可以表示为

$$
R _ {n} (\delta) \approx 1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}}, \quad R _ {n} ^ {\dagger} (\delta) \approx 1 + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}}.
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {f} ^ {\prime} = \hat {R} ^ {\dagger} \hat {f} \hat {R} = \left(1 + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}}\right) \hat {f} \left(1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}}\right) \\ = \hat {f} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} \hat {f} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} \hat {f} \\ = \hat {f} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \left[ \hat {\boldsymbol {L}}, \hat {f} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，只要 $\hat { L }$ 和 $\hat { f }$ 对易，则 $\hat { f } ^ { \prime } = \hat { R } ^ { \dagger } \hat { f } \hat { R } = \hat { f }$ 成立.同理，对于旋转任意角度 $\varphi$ 也成立.

*习题 6.16 从方程 (6.33) 出发，求矢量算符 $\hat { V }$ 绕 $y$ 轴旋转一无限小角度 $\delta$ 的变换，即求矩阵 $_ { D }$

$$
\hat {V} ^ {\prime} = D \hat {V}.
$$

沿着解答 $y$ 轴的无限小角度 $\delta$ 旋转操作为

$$
R _ {y} (\delta) \approx 1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \hat {\boldsymbol {y}} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} = 1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} L _ {y},
$$

$$
R _ {y} ^ {\dagger} (\delta) \approx 1 + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \hat {\mathbf {y}} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} = 1 + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} L _ {y},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {\boldsymbol {V}} ^ {\prime} = R _ {\boldsymbol {y}} ^ {\dagger} (\delta) \hat {\boldsymbol {V}} R _ {\boldsymbol {y}} (\delta) = \left(1 + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} L _ {y}\right) \hat {\boldsymbol {V}} \left(1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} L _ {y}\right) = \hat {\boldsymbol {V}} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left[ L _ {y}, \hat {\boldsymbol {V}} \right] \\ = \left( \begin{array}{c} V _ {x} \\ V _ {y} \\ V _ {z} \end{array} \right) + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left( \begin{array}{c} [ L _ {y}, V _ {x} ] \\ [ L _ {y}, V _ {y} ] \\ [ L _ {y}, V _ {z} ] \end{array} \right) \\ = \left( \begin{array}{c} V _ {x} \\ V _ {y} \\ V _ {z} \end{array} \right) + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left( \begin{array}{c} - \mathrm {i} \hbar V _ {z} \\ 0 \\ \mathrm {i} \hbar V _ {x} \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c} V _ {x} + \delta V _ {z} \\ V _ {y} \\ - \delta V _ {x} + V _ {z} \end{array} \right), \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，

$$
\boldsymbol {D} \left(\delta\right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 1 & 0 & \delta \\ 0 & 1 & 0 \\ - \delta & 0 & 1 \end{array} \right).
$$

对比方程 (6.30) (6.31) ，得

$$
\boldsymbol {D} \left(\varphi\right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} \cos \varphi & 0 & \sin \varphi \\ 0 & 1 & 0 \\ - \sin \varphi & 0 & \cos \varphi \end{array} \right).
$$

习题 6.17 考虑以 $^ { \pmb { n } }$ 为转轴的一个无限小旋转操作作用在角动量本征态 $\psi _ { n \ell m }$ 上.证明:

$$
\hat {R} _ {\pmb {n}} (\delta) \psi_ {n \ell m} = \sum_ {m ^ {\prime}} D _ {m ^ {\prime} m} \psi_ {n \ell m ^ {\prime}},
$$

并求复数 $D _ { m ^ { \prime } m }$ (它们依赖于 $\delta , \pmb { n }$ 和 $\ell$ 以及 $m$ 和 $m ^ { \prime }$ . 这个结果很合理:旋转操作并不改变角动量的大小(由 $\ell$ 决定)，但改变其在 $z$ 轴方向的投影(由 $m$ 决定).

证明

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {R} _ {\boldsymbol {n}} (\delta) \psi_ {n \ell m} = \left(1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \hat {\boldsymbol {L}}\right) \psi_ {n \ell m} \\ = \left(1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} n _ {x} L _ {x} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} n _ {y} L _ {y} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} n _ {z} L _ {z}\right) \psi_ {n \ell m} \\ = \psi_ {n \ell m} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left(n _ {x} L _ {x} + n _ {y} L _ {y} + n _ {z} L _ {z}\right) \psi_ {n \ell m} \\ = \psi_ {n \ell m} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left[ n _ {x} \frac {L _ {+} + L _ {-}}{2} + n _ {y} \frac {- \mathrm {i} (L _ {+} - L _ {-})}{2} + n _ {z} L _ {z} \right] \psi_ {n \ell m} \\ = \psi_ {n \ell m} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left(\frac {n _ {x} - \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} L _ {+} + \frac {n _ {x} + \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} L _ {-} + n _ {z} L _ {z}\right) \psi_ {n \ell m} \\ = \psi_ {n \ell m} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left(\frac {n _ {x} - \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} L _ {+} + \frac {n _ {x} + \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} L _ {-} + n _ {z} L _ {z}\right) \psi_ {n \ell m} \\ = \psi_ {n \ell m} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left[ \frac {n _ {x} - \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} \hbar \sqrt {\ell (\ell + 1) - m (m + 1)} \psi_ {n \ell m + 1} \right. \\ \left. + \frac {n _ {x} + \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} \hbar \sqrt {\ell (\ell + 1) - m (m - 1)} \psi_ {n \ell m - 1} + n _ {z} m \hbar \psi_ {n \ell m} \right] \\ = \left(1 - \mathrm {i} \delta m n _ {z}\right) \psi_ {n \ell m} - \mathrm {i} \delta \frac {n _ {x} - \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} \sqrt {\ell (\ell + 1) - m (m + 1)} \psi_ {n \ell m + 1} \\ - \mathrm {i} \delta \frac {n _ {x} + \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} \sqrt {\ell (\ell + 1) - m (m - 1)} \psi_ {n \ell m - 1} \\ = \sum_ {m ^ {\prime}} D _ {m ^ {\prime} m} \psi_ {n \ell m ^ {\prime}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} D _ {m ^ {\prime} m} = \left(1 - \mathrm {i} \delta m n _ {z}\right) \delta_ {m ^ {\prime}, m} - \mathrm {i} \delta \frac {n _ {x} - \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} \sqrt {\ell (\ell + 1) - m (m + 1)} \delta_ {m ^ {\prime}, m + 1} \\ - \mathrm {i} \delta \frac {n _ {x} + \mathrm {i} n _ {y}}{2} \sqrt {\ell (\ell + 1) - m (m - 1)} \delta_ {m ^ {\prime}, m - 1}. \\ \end{array}
$$

习题 6.18 考虑一维自由粒子: $\hat { H } = \hat { p } ^ { 2 } / ( 2 m )$ 该哈密顿量同时具有平移和反演对称性.

(a) 证明:平移对称性和反演对称性不对易.   
(b) 根据平移对称性 $\hat { H }$ 的本征态可以作为动量的共同本征态，即 $f _ { p } \left( x \right)$ {方程(3.32)). 证明:如果宇称算符使得 $f _ { p } \left( x \right)$ 变为 $f _ { - p } \left( x \right)$ 则这两个态必须具有相同的能量.

(c) 相应地，根据反演对称性 $\hat { H }$ 的本征态可以作为字称的共同本征态，即

$$
\frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \cos \left(\frac {p x}{\hbar}\right) \text {和} \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \sin \left(\frac {p x}{\hbar}\right).
$$

证明:平移算符将这两个态混合在一起;因此，它们必须是简井的，

注释宇称和平移不变性对于解释自由粒子光谱的简并性都是需要的，如果没有宇称， $f _ { p } \left( x \right)$ 和 $f _ { - p } \left( x \right)$ 具有相同的能量就不成立(并不是说完全基于现在对对称性的讨论..... .很明显，可以把它和定态薛定湾方程联系起来，证明它是正确的).

证明 (时引入试探函数 $f \left( x \right)$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {\Pi}, \hat {T} \right] f (x) = \hat {\Pi} \hat {T} f (x) - \hat {T} \hat {\Pi} f (x) \\ = \hat {\Pi} f (x - a) - \hat {T} f (- x) \\ = f (- x - a) - f [ - (x - a) ] \\ = f (- x - a) - f (- x + a) \neq 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

字称算符作用在动量本征态上

$$
\hat {\Pi} f _ {p} (x) = \hat {\Pi} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p (- x) / \hbar} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} = f _ {- p} (x).
$$

(c) 平移算符 $\hat { T }$ 分别作用在本征态 ${ \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { \pi \hbar } } } \cos \left( { \frac { p x } { \hbar } } \right)$ 和 ${ \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { \pi \hbar } } } \sin \left( { \frac { p x } { \hbar } } \right)$ 上，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {T} \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \cos \left(\frac {p x}{\hbar}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \cos \left[ \frac {p (x - a)}{\hbar} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \cos \left(\frac {p x}{\hbar}\right) \cos \left(\frac {p a}{\hbar}\right) + \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \sin \left(\frac {p x}{\hbar}\right) \sin \left(\frac {p a}{\hbar}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {T} \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \sin \left(\frac {p x}{\hbar}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \sin \left[ \frac {p (x - a)}{\hbar} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \sin \left(\frac {p x}{\hbar}\right) \cos \left(\frac {p a}{\hbar}\right) - \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar}} \cos \left(\frac {p x}{\hbar}\right) \sin \left(\frac {p a}{\hbar}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

困此，平移算符 $\hat { T }$ 使两个本征态混合在一起.

习题 6.19 对于任意矢量算符 $\hat { V }$ ，可以定义升降阶算符

$$
\hat {V} _ {\pm} = \hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y}.
$$

(a) 利用方程 (6.33) ，证明:

$$
\left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] = \pm \hbar \hat {V} _ {+}.
$$

$$
\left[ \hat {L} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] = 2 \hbar^ {2} \hat {V} _ {\pm} \pm 2 \hbar \hat {V} _ {\pm} \hat {L} _ {z} \mp 2 \hbar \hat {V} _ {z} \hat {L} _ {\pm}.
$$

(b) 证明:如果 $\psi$ 为 ${ \hat { L } } ^ { 2 }$ 和 $\hat { L } _ { z }$ 的本征态，对应的本征值分别为 $\ell \left( \ell + 1 \right) \hbar ^ { 2 }$ 和 $\ell \hbar$ 则在 $\hat { V } _ { + } \psi$ 要么为萃，要么也是 ${ \hat { L } } ^ { 2 }$ 和 $\hat { L } _ { z }$ 的本征态，对应的本征值分别为

$( \ell + 1 ) ( \ell + 2 ) \hbar ^ { 2 }$ 和 $( \ell + 1 ) \hbar$ 这意味着算符 $\hat { V } _ { + }$ 作用在具有最大值 $m _ { \ell } = \ell$ 的态时，该态的量子数 $\ell$ 和 $m$ 要么同时提升 ，要么被破坏.

证明(帧程 (6.3 $\left[ \hat { L } _ { i } , \hat { V } _ { j } \right] = \mathrm { i } \hbar \varepsilon _ { i j k } \hat { V } _ { k }$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] = \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y} \right] = \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {y} \pm \mathrm {i} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {x}\right) = \pm \hbar \left(\hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y}\right) = \pm \hbar \hat {V} _ {\pm}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {L} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] = \left[ L _ {x} ^ {2} + L _ {y} ^ {2} + L _ {z} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] = \left[ L _ {x} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] + \left[ L _ {y} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] + \left[ L _ {z} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] \\ = \left[ L _ {x} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y} \right] + \left[ L _ {y} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y} \right] + \left[ L _ {z} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] \\ = \left[ L _ {x} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ L _ {x} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] + \left[ L _ {y} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ L _ {y} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] + \left[ L _ {z} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] \\ = 0 \pm \mathrm {i} L _ {x} \left[ L _ {x}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ L _ {x}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] L _ {x} + L _ {y} \left[ L _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] + \left[ L _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] L _ {y} \\ + L _ {z} \left[ L _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] + \left[ L _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] L _ {z} \\ = \pm \mathrm {i} \left(\mathrm {i} \hbar L _ {x} V _ {z} + \mathrm {i} \hbar V _ {z} L _ {x}\right) - \mathrm {i} \hbar L _ {y} V _ {z} - \mathrm {i} \hbar V _ {z} L _ {y} \pm \hbar L _ {z} V _ {\pm} \pm \hbar V _ {\pm} L _ {z} \\ = \left(\mp \hbar L _ {x} - \mathrm {i} \hbar L _ {y}\right) V _ {z} + V _ {z} \left(\mp \hbar L _ {x} - \mathrm {i} \hbar L _ {y}\right) \pm \hbar L _ {z} V _ {\pm} \pm \hbar V _ {\pm} L _ {z} \\ = \mp \hbar (L _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} L _ {y}) V _ {z} \mp \hbar V _ {z} (L _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} L _ {y}) \pm \hbar L _ {z} V _ {\pm} \pm \hbar V _ {\pm} L _ {z} \\ = \mp \hbar L _ {\pm} V _ {z} \mp \hbar V _ {z} L _ {\pm} \pm \hbar L _ {z} V _ {\pm} \pm \hbar V _ {\pm} L _ {z}. \\ \end{array}
$$

将平民 土飞=平民冗 土土民 [v..， 土J，土ñL"V;土=土削包 "， [v; "，] 代入上式，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mp \hbar L _ {\pm} V _ {z} \mp \hbar V _ {z} L _ {\pm} \pm \hbar L _ {z} V _ {\pm} \pm \hbar V _ {\pm} L _ {z} \\ = \pm \hbar \left[ V _ {z}, L _ {\pm} \right] \mp 2 \hbar V _ {z} L _ {\pm} \pm 2 \hbar V _ {\pm} L _ {z} \mp \left[ V _ {\pm}, L _ {z} \right] \\ = \pm \hbar \left(\left[ V _ {z}, L _ {x} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ V _ {z}, L _ {y} \right]\right) \mp 2 \hbar V _ {z} L _ {\pm} \pm 2 \hbar V _ {\pm} L _ {z} + \hbar^ {2} V _ {\pm} \\ = 2 \hbar^ {2} V _ {\pm} \pm 2 \hbar V _ {\pm} L _ {z} \mp 2 \hbar V _ {z} L _ {\pm}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 满足己知条件的本征态为 $\psi _ { n \ell \ell }$ .

将对易关系 $\left[ \hat { L } _ { z } , \hat { V } _ { + } \right] = \hbar \hat { V } _ { + }$ 和 $\left[ \hat { L } ^ { 2 } , \hat { V } _ { + } \right] = 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } \hat { V } _ { + } + 2 \hbar \hat { V } _ { + } \hat { L } _ { z } - 2 \hbar \hat { V } _ { z } \hat { L } _ { + }$ 分别作用到被函数 $\psi _ { n \ell \ell }$ 上，得

$$
\left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {+} \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \hbar \hat {V} _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell},
$$

$$
L _ {z} V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} - V _ {+} L _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \hbar \hat {V} _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell},
$$

$$
L _ {z} \left(V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right) = \hbar \hat {V} _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + \ell \hbar V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = (\ell + 1) \hbar \left(\hat {V} _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right).
$$

$$
\left[ \hat {L} ^ {2}, \hat {V} _ {+} \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left(2 \hbar^ {2} \hat {V} _ {+} + 2 \hbar \hat {V} _ {+} \hat {L} _ {z} - 2 \hbar \hat {V} _ {z} \hat {L} _ {+}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell},
$$

$$
L ^ {2} V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} - V _ {+} L ^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = 2 \hbar^ {2} V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + 2 \hbar V _ {+} L _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} - 2 \hbar V _ {z} L _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell},
$$

$$
L ^ {2} \left(V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right) - V _ {+} \ell (\ell + 1) \hbar^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = 2 \hbar^ {2} V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + 2 \ell \hbar^ {2} V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} - 2 \hbar V _ {z} L _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell},
$$

$$
L ^ {2} \left(V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right) = \left[ \ell (\ell + 1) + 2 + 2 \ell \right] \hbar^ {2} \left(V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right),
$$

$$
L ^ {2} \left(V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right) = (\ell + 1) (\ell + 2) \hbar^ {2} \left(V _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right).
$$

由此可见， $V _ { + }$ +作用在被函数 $\psi _ { n \ell \ell }$ 上使得 $L _ { z }$ 的本征值为 $( \ell + 1 ) \hbar$ , $L ^ { 2 }$ 的本征值为 $( \ell + 1 ) \times ( \ell + 2 ) \hbar ^ { 2 }$ .

习题 6.20 证明:对易关 $\left[ { \hat { L } } _ { - } , { \hat { f } } \right] = 0$ $\left[ \hat { L } _ { + } , \hat { f } \right] = 0$ 会导致形如方程 (6.46)的同样的定则.

证明 动量本征态的左矢 $\langle n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } |$ 和右矢 $| n \ell m \rangle$ 分别左乘和右乘到对易关系 $\left[ { \hat { L } } _ { - } , { \hat { f } } \right] = 0$ 的两边，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ \hat {L} _ {-}, \hat {f} \right] | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {-} \hat {f} - \hat {f} \hat {L} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {-} \hat {f} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {f} \hat {L} _ {-} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \hat {L} _ {+} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {f} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {f} \left| \hat {L} _ {-} n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = \hbar \sqrt {\ell^ {\prime} (\ell^ {\prime} + 1) - m ^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} + 1)} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} + 1 \right| \hat {f} | n \ell m \rangle \\ - \hbar \sqrt {\ell (\ell + 1) - m (m - 1)} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {f} | n \ell m - 1 \rangle \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

在矩阵元不等于零的情况下，它们的系数相等且两个矩阵元相等.这要求 $\ell = \ell ^ { \prime } , m = m ^ { \prime } + 1$ ，以及

$$
\left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell m \right| \hat {f} | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell (m - 1) \right| \hat {f} | n \ell (m - 1) \rangle ,
$$

这与方程 (6 .46) 的情况一致.

申习题 6.21 电子处于氢原子态

$$
\psi = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\psi_ {2 1 1} + \psi_ {2 1 - 1}\right),
$$

先用一个约化矩阵元来表示，然后再求 $\langle r \rangle$ .

解答坐标期望值

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle r \rangle = \langle \psi | r | \psi \rangle = \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 1} + \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 1} + \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 1} \right\rangle + \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle \psi_ {2 1 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 1} \right\rangle\right) + \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 1} \right\rangle + \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle \psi_ {2 1 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right\rangle^ {*}\right) + \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 1} \right\rangle + \operatorname {R e} \left(\left\langle \psi_ {2 1 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right\rangle\right) + \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 - 1} \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

由方程 (6 .47)

$$
\left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {f} | n \ell m \rangle = \delta_ {\ell \ell^ {\prime}} \delta_ {m m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell \| f \| n \ell \right\rangle ,
$$

可得

$$
\langle r \rangle = \left\langle \psi_ {2 1} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1} \right\rangle .
$$

将 $m$ 取为零便于积分，得

$$
\langle r \rangle = \left\langle \psi_ {2 1 0} \right| r \left| \psi_ {2 1 0} \right\rangle = \int \psi_ {2 1 0} ^ {*} (r) r \psi_ {2 1 0} ^ {*} (r) d ^ {3} r = 5 a.
$$

# *习题 6.22

(a) 证明:方程 (6.50)"'(6.54) 的对易关系符合矢量算符的定义(方程 (6.33)).

如果你做了习题 6.19 ，已经推导了其中的一个.

(b) 推导方程 (6.57).

解答 (a)

$$
\left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {z} \right] = \mathrm {i} \hbar \varepsilon_ {z z k} \hat {V} _ {k} = 0.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] = \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y} \right] = \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] = \mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {y} \pm \mathrm {i} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {x}\right) \\ = \pm \hbar (\hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y}) = \pm \hbar \hat {V} _ {\pm}. \tag {6.51} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {L} _ {\pm}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] = \left[ \hat {L} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {L} _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y} \right] \\ = \left[ \hat {L} _ {x}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ \hat {L} _ {x}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ \hat {L} _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] - \left[ \hat {L} _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] \\ = 0 \pm \mathrm {i} \left(\mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {z}\right) \pm \mathrm {i} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {z}\right) - 0 \\ = \mp \hbar \hat {V} _ {z} \pm \hbar \hat {V} _ {z} \\ = 0. \tag {6.52} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {L} _ {\pm}, \hat {V} _ {z} \right] = \left[ \hat {L} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {L} _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {z} \right] = \left[ \hat {L} _ {x}, \hat {V} _ {z} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ \hat {L} _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {z} \right] \\ = \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {y}\right) \pm \mathrm {i} \left(\mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {x}\right) = \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {y}\right) \mp \left(\hbar \hat {V} _ {x}\right) \\ = \mp \hbar \left(\hat {V} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y}\right) \\ = \mp \hbar \hat {V} _ {\pm}. \tag {6.53} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {L} _ {\pm}, \hat {V} _ {\mp} \right] = \left[ \hat {L} _ {x} \pm \mathrm {i} \hat {L} _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {x} \mp \mathrm {i} \hat {V} _ {y} \right] \\ = \left[ \hat {L} _ {x}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] \mp \mathrm {i} \left[ \hat {L} _ {x}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] \pm \mathrm {i} \left[ \hat {L} _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {x} \right] + \left[ \hat {L} _ {y}, \hat {V} _ {y} \right] \\ = 0 \mp \mathrm {i} \left(\mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {z}\right) \pm \mathrm {i} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \hat {V} _ {z}\right) + 0 \\ = \pm 2 \hbar \hat {V} _ {z}. \tag {6.54} \\ \end{array}
$$

(b)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ \hat {L} _ {z}, \hat {V} _ {z} \right] | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {z} \hat {V} _ {z} - \hat {V} _ {z} \hat {L} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {z} \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \hat {L} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = m ^ {\prime} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle - m \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = \left(m ^ {\prime} - m\right) \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

除非 $m ^ { \prime } - m = 0$ 否则 $\langle n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } | \hat { V } _ { z } | n \ell m \rangle = 0$ .

习题 6.23 方程 (4.183) 定义了 CG 系数.按照下式将两个角动量分别为 $j _ { 1 }$ 和 $j _ { 2 }$ 的态组合成一个总角动量为 $J$ 的态:

$$
| J M \rangle = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} | j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \rangle .
$$

(a) 从方程 (6.64) 出发，证明: CG 系数满足

$$
C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} = \langle j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} | J M \rangle .
$$

(b) $\hat { J } _ { \pm } = \hat { J } _ { \pm } ^ { ( 1 ) } + \hat { J } _ { \pm } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ 应用到方程 (6.64) 中，推导 CG 系数的递归关系:

$$
A _ {J} ^ {M} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M + 1} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} = B _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} C _ {m _ {1} - 1 m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} + B _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} - 1 M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J},
$$

$$
B _ {J} ^ {M} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M - 1} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} = A _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} C _ {m + 1 m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} + A _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} + 1 M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J}.
$$

解答 (时利用正交性关系

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle j _ {1}   j _ {2}   m _ {1} ^ {\prime}   m ^ {\prime} | J M \right\rangle = \left\langle j _ {1}   j _ {2}   m _ {1} ^ {\prime}   m _ {2} ^ {\prime} \left| \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2}   M} ^ {j _ {1}   j _ {2}   J} \right| j _ {1}   j _ {2}   m _ {1}   m _ {2} \right\rangle \\ = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left\langle j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} ^ {\prime} m _ {2} ^ {\prime} | j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \right\rangle \\ = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} m ^ {\prime} _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \delta_ {m _ {1}, m ^ {\prime} _ {1}} \delta_ {m _ {2}, m ^ {\prime} _ {2}} \\ = C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) $\hat { J } _ { \pm } = \hat { J } _ { \pm } ^ { ( 1 ) } + \hat { J } _ { \pm } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ 作用到教材中方程 (6剧)，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {J} _ {\pm} | J M \rangle = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left(\hat {J} _ {\pm} ^ {(1)} + \hat {J} _ {\pm} ^ {(2)}\right) | j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \rangle \\ = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ \hat {J} _ {\pm} ^ {(1)} | j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \rangle + \hat {J} _ {\pm} ^ {(2)} | j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \rangle \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

下标取正号时，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {J} _ {+} | J M \rangle = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ \hat {J} _ {+} ^ {(1)} | j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \rangle + \hat {J} _ {+} ^ {(2)} | j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \rangle \right], \\ A _ {J} ^ {M} | J M + 1 \rangle = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ A _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} | j _ {1} j _ {2} (m _ {1} + 1) m _ {2} \rangle + A _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} | j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} (m _ {2} + 1) \rangle \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

用左矢( $\left. j _ { 1 } j _ { 2 } m _ { \textrm { 1 } } ^ { \prime } m _ { \textrm { 2 } } ^ { \prime } \right|$ 左乘上式两边并结合 (a) 中结论，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} ^ {\prime} m _ {2} ^ {\prime} \right| A _ {J} ^ {M} | J M + 1 \rangle = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ \left\langle j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} ^ {\prime} m _ {2} ^ {\prime} \right| A _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} | j _ {1} j _ {2} (m _ {1} + 1) m _ {2} \right\rangle \\ \left. \left. + \left\langle j _ {1} j _ {2} m ^ {\prime} _ {1} m ^ {\prime} _ {2} \right| A _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} \mid j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} (m _ {2} + 1) \right\rangle \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
A _ {J} ^ {M} C _ {m _ {1} ^ {\prime} m _ {2} ^ {\prime}} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} _ {M + 1} = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ A _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} \delta_ {m _ {1} + 1, m _ {1} ^ {\prime}} \delta_ {m _ {2}, m _ {2} ^ {\prime}} + A _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} \delta_ {m _ {1}, m _ {1} ^ {\prime}} \delta_ {m _ {2} + 1, m _ {2} ^ {\prime}} \right].
$$

等式右边中括号内第一项不为零时，要求 $m _ { 1 } + 1 = m _ { \ 1 } ^ { \prime } , m _ { 2 } = m _ { \ 2 } ^ { \prime }$ 第二项不为零时，要求 $m _ { 1 } + 1 =$ $m _ { 1 } ^ { \prime } , m _ { 2 } = m _ { \ 2 } ^ { \prime }$ 因此，

$$
A _ {J} ^ {M} C _ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} m ^ {\prime} _ {2} M + 1} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} = A _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} - 1} C _ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} - 1 m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} + A _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m ^ {\prime} _ {2} - 1} C _ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} m _ {2} - 1 M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J}.
$$

令 $A _ { j } ^ { m - 1 } = B _ { j } ^ { m }$ ，上式可写为

$$
A _ {J} ^ {M} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M + 1} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} = B _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} C _ {m _ {1} - 1 m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} + B _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} - 1 M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J}.
$$

取负号时，

$$
\hat {J} _ {-} \left| J M \right\rangle = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ \hat {J} _ {-} ^ {(1)} \left| j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \right\rangle + \hat {J} _ {-} ^ {(2)} \left| j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} m _ {2} \right\rangle \right],
$$

$$
B _ {J} ^ {M} \left| J M - 1 \right\rangle = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ B _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} \left| j _ {1} j _ {2} (m _ {1} - 1) m _ {2} \right\rangle + B _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} \left| j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} (m _ {2} - 1) \right\rangle \right].
$$

同样，用左矢( $\left. j _ { 1 } j _ { 2 } m _ { \textrm { 1 } } ^ { \prime } m _ { \textrm { 2 } } ^ { \prime } \right|$ 左乘上式两边并结合(a.)中结论，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle j _ {1}   j _ {2}   m _ {1} ^ {\prime}   m _ {2} ^ {\prime} \right| B _ {J} ^ {M} | J M - 1 \rangle = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ \left\langle j _ {1}   j _ {2}   m _ {1} ^ {\prime}   m _ {2} ^ {\prime} \right| B _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} \left| j _ {1}   j _ {2}   (m _ {1} - 1)   m _ {2} \right\rangle \right. \\ \left. + \left\langle j _ {1} j _ {2} m ^ {\prime} _ {1} m ^ {\prime} _ {2} \right| B _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} \left| j _ {1} j _ {2} m _ {1} (m _ {2} - 1) \right\rangle \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
B _ {J} ^ {M} C _ {m _ {1} ^ {\prime} m _ {2} ^ {\prime}} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} _ {M - 1} = \sum_ {m _ {1}, m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} \left[ B _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} \delta_ {m _ {1} - 1, m _ {1} ^ {\prime}} \delta_ {m _ {2}, m _ {2} ^ {\prime}} + B _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} \delta_ {m _ {1}, m _ {1} ^ {\prime}} \delta_ {m _ {2} - 1, m _ {2} ^ {\prime}} \right].
$$

等式右边中括号内第一项不为零时，要求 $m _ { 1 } - 1 = { m ^ { \prime } } _ { 1 } , m _ { 2 } = { m ^ { \prime } } _ { 2 }$ . 第三项不为零时，要求 $m _ { 1 } =$ $m _ { \textrm { 1 } , m _ { 2 } \textrm { - } 1 } ^ { \prime } = m _ { \textrm { 2 } } ^ { \prime }$ . 因此，

$$
B _ {J} ^ {M} C _ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} m ^ {\prime} _ {2}} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} M - 1 = B _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} + 1} C _ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} + 1 m ^ {\prime} _ {2}} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} M + B _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m ^ {\prime} _ {2} + 1} C _ {m ^ {\prime} _ {1} m ^ {\prime} _ {2} + 1 M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J}.
$$

$B _ { j } ^ { m + 1 } = A _ { j } ^ { m }$ ，上式可以写为

$$
B _ {J} ^ {M} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} M - 1} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} = A _ {j _ {1}} ^ {m _ {1}} C _ {m _ {1} + 1 m _ {2} M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J} + A _ {j _ {2}} ^ {m _ {2}} C _ {m _ {1} m _ {2} + 1 M} ^ {j _ {1} j _ {2} J}.
$$

# 事**习题 6.24

(a) 将方程 (6.52) $\sim$ (6.54) 中的六个对易关系放在态 $\langle n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } |$ 和 $| n \ell m \rangle$ 中间，从而得到 $\hat { V }$ 的矩阵元之间的关系.例如，升阶算符由方程 (6.52) 给出:

$$
B _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} - 1) \right| V _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle = A _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| V _ {+} | n \ell (m + 1) \rangle .
$$

(b) 利用习题 6.23 中的结论，证明 (a) 部分的六个表达式满足方程 $( 6 . 5 9 ) \sim$ (6.61).

解答(刊程 (6.5 $\left[ \hat { L } _ { \pm } , \hat { V } _ { \pm } \right] = 0$

对于指标取正的情况，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ \hat {L} _ {+}, \hat {V} _ {+} \right] | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {+} \hat {V} _ {+} - \hat {V} _ {+} \hat {L} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {+} \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} \hat {L} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle \hat {L} _ {-} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} \left| \hat {L} _ {+} n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = B _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} \left(m ^ {\prime} - 1\right) \right| \hat {V} _ {+} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle - A _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} \left| n \ell (m + 1) \right\rangle \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

即 $B _ { \ell ^ { \prime } } ^ { m ^ { \prime } } \langle n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } ( m ^ { \prime } - 1 ) | \hat { V } _ { + } | n \ell m \rangle = A _ { \ell } ^ { m } \langle n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } | \hat { V } _ { + } | n \ell ( m + 1 ) \rangle .$

对于指标取负的情况，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ \hat {L} _ {-}, \hat {V} _ {-} \right] | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {-} \hat {V} _ {-} - \hat {V} _ {-} \hat {L} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {-} \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} l ^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} \hat {L} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \left\langle \hat {L} _ {+} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} l ^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} \left| \hat {L} _ {-} n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = A _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} + 1) \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle - B _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} l ^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell (m - 1) \rangle \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

即有

$$
A _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} + 1) \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle = B _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell (m - 1) \rangle .
$$

方程 (6 则为 $\left[ \hat { L } _ { \pm } , \hat { V } _ { z } \right] = \mp \hbar \hat { V } _ { \pm }$

对于指标取正的情况，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ \hat {L} _ {+}, \hat {V} _ {z} \right] | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {+} \hat {V} _ {z} - \hat {V} _ {z} \hat {L} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {+} \hat {V} _ {z} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \hat {L} _ {+} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \hat {L} _ {-} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \left| \hat {L} _ {+} n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = B _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} - 1) \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle - A _ {l} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell (m + 1) \rangle \\ = - \hbar \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

即有

$$
B _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} - 1) \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle = A _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell (m + 1) \rangle - \hbar \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle .
$$

对于指标取负的情况，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ \hat {L} _ {-}, \hat {V} _ {z} \right] | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {-} \hat {V} _ {z} - \hat {V} _ {z} \hat {L} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {-} \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \hat {L} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle \hat {L} _ {+} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \left| \hat {L} _ {-} n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = A _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} + 1) \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle - B _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell (m - 1) \rangle \\ = \hbar \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

即有

$$
A _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} + 1) \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle = B _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell (m - 1) \rangle + \hbar \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle .
$$

方程(归阳叫6.54钊)为[ $\left[ \hat { L } _ { \pm } , \hat { V } _ { \mp } \right] = \pm 2 \hbar \hat { V } _ { z }$

对子指标取正的情况'

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ \hat {L} _ {+}, \hat {V} _ {-} \right] | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {+} \hat {V} _ {-} - \hat {V} _ {-} \hat {L} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {+} \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} \hat {L} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle \hat {L} _ {-} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} \left| \hat {L} _ {+} n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = B _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} - 1) \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle - A _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} | n \ell (m + 1) \rangle \\ = 2 \hbar \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

即有

$$
B _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} \left(m ^ {\prime} - 1\right) \right| \hat {V} _ {-} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle = A _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {-} \left| n \ell (m + 1) \right\rangle + 2 \hbar \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} \left| n \ell m \right\rangle .
$$

对于指标取负的情况，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \left[ \hat {L} _ {-}, \hat {V} _ {+} \right] | n \ell m \rangle = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {-} \hat {V} _ {+} - \hat {V} _ {+} \hat {L} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {L} _ {-} \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} \hat {L} _ {-} | n \ell m \rangle \\ = \left\langle \hat {L} _ {+} n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle - \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} \left| \hat {L} _ {-} n \ell m \right\rangle \\ = A _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} (m ^ {\prime} + 1) \right| \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle - B _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell (m - 1) \rangle \\ = - 2 \hbar \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

即有

$$
A _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} \left(m ^ {\prime} + 1\right) \right| \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle = B _ {\ell} ^ {m} \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} \left| n \ell (m - 1) \right\rangle - 2 \hbar \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {z} | n \ell m \rangle .
$$

(b) (a) 个结论如下.

\left[ \hat {L} _ {\pm}, \hat {V} _ {\pm} \right] = 0:

又因为 $A _ { \ell } ^ { m } = \hbar { \sqrt { \ell \left( \ell + 1 \right) - m \left( m + 1 \right) } } .$ ，得 $A _ { 1 } ^ { 1 } = 0$ 所以满足方程 (6.59)"'(6.61).

$$

由方程 (6.66) 的第二式，得

B _ {\ell^ {\prime}} ^ {m ^ {\prime}} C _ {m 0 m ^ {\prime} - 1} ^ {\ell 1 \ell^ {\prime}} = A _ {\ell} ^ {m} C _ {m + 1 0 m ^ {\prime}} ^ {\ell 1 \ell^ {\prime}} + A _ {1} ^ {0} C _ {m 1 m ^ {\prime}} ^ {\ell 1 \ell^ {\prime}}.
$$

又因为

$$
A _ {\ell} ^ {m} = \hbar \sqrt {\ell (\ell + 1) - m (m + 1)}, \text {得} A _ {1} ^ {0} = \sqrt {2} \hbar .
