事习题 6.25 用单个约化矩阵元表示电子的偶极矩 $p _ { \mathrm { e } }$ 在氢原子态

$$
\psi = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\psi_ {2 1 1} + \psi_ {2 0 0}\right)
$$

上的期望值，并估算该期望值.注释这是一个矢量的期望值，因此你需要计算三个分量.不要忘记使用拉液特定则!

解答

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \psi | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | \psi \rangle = \frac {1}{2} \left[ \left(\langle 2 1 1 | + \langle 2 0 0 |\right) \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} (| 2 1 1 \rangle + | 2 0 0 \rangle) \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left[ \langle 2 1 1 | p _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 1 1 \rangle + \langle 2 1 1 | p _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 0 0 \rangle + \langle 2 0 0 | p _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 1 1 \rangle + \langle 2 0 0 | p _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 0 0 \rangle \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left[ \langle 2 1 1 | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 1 1 \rangle + 2 \mathrm {R e} \left(\langle 2 1 1 | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 0 0 \rangle\right) + \langle 2 0 0 | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 0 0 \rangle \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

由拉波特定则得，当 $\ell + \ell ^ { \prime }$ 为偶数时，

$$
\left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| p _ {\mathrm {e}} | n \ell m \rangle = 0.
$$

因此，主式的第一项和第三项为零.

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \psi | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | \psi \rangle = \operatorname {R e} \left(\left\langle 2 1 1 | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | 2 0 0 \right\rangle\right) \\ = - e \mathrm {R e} \left(\langle 2 1 1 | r | 2 0 0 \rangle\right) \\ = - e \mathrm {R e} \left[ \langle 2 1 1 | x | 2 0 0 \rangle \hat {x} + \langle 2 1 1 | y | 2 0 0 \rangle \hat {y} + \langle 2 1 1 | z | 2 0 0 \rangle \hat {z} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

由方程 (6.57) $m ^ { \prime } \neq m$ 时 $\langle n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } | \hat { V } _ { z } | n \ell m \rangle = 0$ 所以上式最后一项为零.

$r _ { + } = x + \mathrm { i } y , r _ { - } = x - \mathrm { i } y ,$ ，贝t $x = { \frac { r _ { + } + r _ { - } } { 2 } } , y = { \frac { r _ { + } - r _ { - } } { 2 i } }$ .， 一伊----;:;-::-二;困此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \psi | \boldsymbol {p} _ {e} | \psi \rangle = - e \operatorname {R e} \left[ \langle 2 1 1 | \frac {r _ {+} + r _ {-}}{2} | 2 0 0 \rangle \hat {x} + \langle 2 1 1 | \frac {r _ {+} - r _ {-}}{2 \mathrm {i}} | 2 0 0 \rangle \hat {y} \right] \\ = - e \operatorname {R e} \left[ \langle 2 1 1 | \frac {r _ {+} \hat {x} - \mathrm {i} r _ {+} \hat {y}}{2} | 2 0 0 \rangle + \langle 2 1 1 | \frac {r _ {-} \hat {x} + \mathrm {i} r _ {-} \hat {y}}{2} | 2 0 0 \rangle \right] \\ = - e \mathrm {R e} \left[ \langle 2 1 1 | r _ {+} | 2 0 0 \rangle \frac {\hat {x} - \mathrm {i} \hat {y}}{2} + \langle 2 1 1 | r _ {-} | 2 0 0 \rangle \frac {\hat {x} + \mathrm {i} \hat {y}}{2} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

由方程 (6.58) ，当 $m ^ { \prime } \neq m - 1$ 时， $\langle n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } | \hat { V } _ { - } | n \ell m \rangle = 0$ ，所以上式关于 $r _ { - }$ 项为零.于是，

$$
\langle \psi | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | \psi \rangle = - e \mathrm {R e} \left[ \langle 2 1 1 | r _ {+} | 2 0 0 \rangle \frac {\hat {x} - \mathrm {i} \hat {y}}{2} \right].
$$

由方程 (6.59)

$$
\left\langle n ^ {\prime} \ell^ {\prime} m ^ {\prime} \right| \hat {V} _ {+} | n \ell m \rangle = - \sqrt {2} C _ {m 1 m ^ {\prime}} ^ {\ell 1 \ell^ {\prime}} \left\langle n \ell^ {\prime} \| V \| n \ell \right\rangle ,
$$

则有

$$
\left\langle 2 1 1 \mid r _ {+} \mid 2 0 0 \right\rangle = - \sqrt {2} C _ {0 1 1} ^ {0 1 1} \left\langle 2 1 \mid r \mid 2 0 \right\rangle .
$$

因此，

$$
\langle \psi | \boldsymbol {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | \psi \rangle = - e \operatorname {R e} \left[ - \sqrt {2} C _ {0 1 1} ^ {0 1 1} \langle 2 1 | r | 2 0 \rangle \frac {\hat {x} - \mathrm {i} \hat {y}}{2} \right].
$$

CG 系数计算器(网址: https:jjwww.wolfra alpha. mjinputj $? \mathrm { i } =$ Clebsch-Gordan+calculator) 可以查得 $C _ { m _ { 1 } m _ { 2 } m } ^ { s _ { 1 } s _ { 2 } s } = C _ { 0 1 1 } ^ { 0 1 1 } = 1$ ，则

$$
\langle \psi | \pmb {p} _ {\mathrm {e}} | \psi \rangle = e \mathrm {R e} \left[ \langle 2 1 | r | 2 0 \rangle \frac {\hat {x} - \mathrm {i} \hat {y}}{\sqrt {2}} \right] = \frac {e}{\sqrt {2}} \langle 2 1 | r | 2 0 \rangle \hat {x}.
$$

又础由方姆程 (阳阳阳6.6创 $\operatorname { l } ) \langle n \ell ^ { \prime } | r | n \ell \rangle = { \frac { 1 } { C _ { m 0 m ^ { \prime } } ^ { \ell 1 \ell ^ { \prime } } } } \left. n ^ { \prime } \ell ^ { \prime } m ^ { \prime } | z | n \ell m \right.$ Ceie ，为简单起见，设 $m = m ^ { \prime } = 0$ ，则

$$
\langle 2 1 | r | 2 0 \rangle = \frac {1}{C _ {0 0 0} ^ {0 1 1}} \langle 2 1 0 | z | 2 0 0 \rangle = \langle 2 1 0 | z | 2 0 0 \rangle .
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \psi | \boldsymbol {p} _ {e} | \psi \rangle = \frac {e}{\sqrt {2}} \langle 2 1 0 | z | 2 0 0 \rangle \hat {x} = \frac {e}{\sqrt {2}} \int \psi_ {2 1 0} ^ {*} r \cos \theta \psi_ {2 0 0} ^ {*} d ^ {3} r \\ = \frac {e}{\sqrt {2}} \left\langle R _ {2 1} \right| r \left| R _ {2 0} \right\rangle \frac {1}{\sqrt {3}} = \frac {e}{\sqrt {2}} \left(- 3 \sqrt {3} a _ {0}\right) \frac {1}{\sqrt {3}} \\ = \frac {- 3 a _ {0} e}{\sqrt {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

串习题 6.26 求例题 6.7 中系统的 $\hat { p } _ { H } \left( t \right)$ 并讨论它与经典运动方程的对应关系.

解答

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {H} (t) = U ^ {\dagger} p U \psi_ {n} (x) \\ = U ^ {\dagger} \left[ \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} (a _ {+} - a _ {-}) \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {\mathrm {i} H t}{\hbar}} \psi_ {n} (x) \\ = U ^ {\dagger} \left[ \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} (a _ {+} - a _ {-}) \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {\mathrm {i} E _ {n} t}{\hbar}} \psi_ {n} (x) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = U ^ {\dagger} \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {\mathrm {i} E _ {n} t}{\hbar}} \left(a _ {+} - a _ {-}\right) \psi_ {n} (x) \\ = U ^ {\dagger} \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {\mathrm {i} E _ {n} t}{\hbar}} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} \psi_ {n + 1} (x) - \sqrt {n} \psi_ {n - 1} (x) \right] \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {\mathrm {i} E _ {n} t}{\hbar}} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} U ^ {\dagger} \psi_ {n + 1} (x) - \sqrt {n} U ^ {\dagger} \psi_ {n - 1} (x) \right] \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {\mathrm {i} E _ {n} t}{\hbar}} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {\mathrm {i} H t}{\hbar}} \psi_ {n + 1} (x) - \sqrt {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {\mathrm {i} H t}{\hbar}} \psi_ {n - 1} (x) \right] \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {\mathrm {i} E _ {n} t}{\hbar}} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {\mathrm {i} E _ {n + 1} t}{\hbar}} \psi_ {n + 1} (x) - \sqrt {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {\frac {\mathrm {i} E _ {n} t}{\hbar}} \psi_ {n - 1} (x) \right] \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t} \psi_ {n + 1} (x) - \sqrt {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \psi_ {n - 1} (x) \right] \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t} a _ {+} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} a _ {-}\right) \psi_ {n}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {H} (t) = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t} a _ {+} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} a _ {-}\right) \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega \hbar}{2}} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \hbar m \omega}} \left(- \mathrm {i} p + m \omega x\right) - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \hbar m \omega}} \left(\mathrm {i} p + m \omega x\right) \right] \\ = p \cos (\omega t) - m \omega x \sin (\omega t). \\ \end{array}
$$

算符写成海森伯绘景的形式

$$
p _ {H} (t) = p _ {H} (0) \cos (\omega t) - m \omega x _ {H} (0) \sin (\omega t),
$$

这与经典方程类似.

事*习题 6.27 考虑质量为 $m$ 的自由粒子.证明:位置和动量算符在海森伯绘景下为

$$
\hat {x} _ {H} (t) = \hat {x} _ {H} (0) + \frac {1}{m} \hat {p} _ {H} (0) t,
$$

$$
\hat {p} _ {H} (t) = \hat {p} _ {H} (0).
$$

讨论这些方程和经典运动方程之间的关系提示 你首先需要估算对易关系 $\left[ \hat { x } , \hat { H } ^ { n } \right]$ 这可以让你估算出对易关系 $\left[ \hat { x } , \hat { U } \right]$

$H = { \frac { p ^ { 2 } } { 2 m } }$ n2证明 自由粒子的哈密醺量

对易关系

$$
\begin{array}{l} [ p, H ] = \left[ p, \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \right] = 0. \\ [ x, H ] = \left[ x, \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \right] = \frac {1}{2 m} \left[ x, p ^ {2} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} p [ x, p ] + \frac {1}{2 m} [ x, p ] p \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} [ x, H ^ {2} ] = H [ x, H ] + [ x, H ] H \\ = H \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p + \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H \\ = 2 \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} [ x, H ^ {3} ] = H [ x, H ^ {2} ] + [ x, H ] H ^ {2} \\ = H 2 \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H + \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H ^ {2} \\ = 3 \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H. \\ \end{array}
$$

由数学归纳法，猜想 $[ x , H ^ { n } ] = n \frac { \mathrm { i } \hbar } { m } p H ^ { n - 1 }$ 1, pH

$n = 1$ 时，显然成立.假设 $n = k$ 时成立，证明 $n = k + 1$ 时也成立即可.

$$
\left[ x, H ^ {k} \right] = k {\frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m}} p H ^ {k - 1}   \text {成 立}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ x, H ^ {k + 1} \right] = H \left[ x, H ^ {k} \right] + [ x, H ] H ^ {k} \\ = H \left(k \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H ^ {k - 1}\right) + \left(\frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p\right) H ^ {k} \\ = k \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H ^ {k} + \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H ^ {k} \\ = (k + 1) \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H ^ {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此， $[ x , H ^ { n } ] = n \frac { \mathrm { i } \hbar } { m } p H ^ { n - 1 }$ Hn-l 成立

求对易关系

$$
\begin{array}{l} [ x, U ] = \left[ x, \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{n !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} H t\right) ^ {n} \right] \\ = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{n !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} t\right) ^ {n} [ x, H ^ {n} ] \\ = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{n !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} t\right) ^ {n} n \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p H ^ {n - 1} \\ = \frac {p t}{m} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{(n - 1) !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} t\right) ^ {n - 1} H ^ {n - 1} \\ = \frac {p t}{m} U. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此 XH (t) = UtxU = ut (Ux+ [川)=x+U $x _ { H } \left( t \right) = U ^ { \dagger } x U = U ^ { \dagger } \left( U x + \left[ x , U \right] \right) = x + U ^ { \dagger } \frac { p t } { m } U = x + \frac { p t } { m }$ 又因

$$
[ p, U ] = 0,
$$

$$
p _ {H} (t) = U ^ {\dagger} p U = U ^ {\dagger} (U p + [ p, U ]) = U ^ {\dagger} U p = p.
$$

用算符写成海森伯绘景的形式，则

$$
\hat {x} _ {H} (t) = \hat {x} _ {H} (0) + \frac {\hat {p} _ {H} (0) t}{m},
$$

$$
\hat {p} _ {H} (t) = \hat {p} _ {H} (0).
$$

习题 6.28 证明:方程 (6.75) (6.76) 是无穷小时间 下薛定诗方程的解.提示对 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ 做泰勒展开.

证明 对披函数 $\varPsi \left( x , t _ { 0 } + \delta \right)$ 在 $t _ { 0 }$ 处进行泰勒展开，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Psi \left(x, t _ {0} + \delta\right) = \Psi \left(x, t _ {0}\right) + \delta \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t} \bigg | _ {t _ {0}} + \dots \\ = \varPsi (x, t _ {0}) + \delta \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} \left(\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t}\right) \varPsi (x, t) | _ {t _ {0}} + \dots \\ = \Psi (x, t _ {0}) + \delta \frac {1}{i \hbar} H \Psi (x, t _ {0}) + \dots \\ = \left[ 1 - \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} H (t _ {0}) + \dots \right] \Psi (x, t _ {0}). \\ \end{array}
$$

结合方程 (6.75) $\varPsi \left( x , t \right) = U \left( t , t _ { 0 } \right) \varPsi \left( x , t _ { 0 } \right)$ ，可以得出

$$
U \left(t _ {0} + \delta , t _ {0}\right) \approx 1 - \frac {\mathrm {i} \delta}{\hbar} H \left(t _ {0}\right).
$$

事习题 6.29 对方程 (6.72) 求微分得到海森伯运动方程

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \hat {Q} _ {H} (t) = [ \hat {Q} _ {H} (t), \hat {H} ]
$$

( $\hat { Q }$ 和 $\hat { H }$ 和时间无关).①插入 $\hat { Q } = \hat { x }$ 和 $\hat { Q } = \hat { p }$ ，在海森伯绘景下，求出质量为 $m$ 的单粒子在势场 $V \left( x \right)$ 中运动的 $\scriptstyle { \hat { x } } _ { H }$ 和 $\hat { p } _ { H }$ 的微分方程.

解警方程 (6.72) $\hat { Q } _ { H } \left( t \right) = \hat { U } ^ { \dagger } \left( t \right) \hat { Q } \hat { U } \left( t \right)$ .

两边做微分可得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} Q _ {H} (t) = \frac {\mathrm {d} U ^ {\dagger} (t)}{\mathrm {d} t} Q U (t) + U ^ {\dagger} (t) Q \frac {\mathrm {d} U (t)}{\mathrm {d} t}
$$

$Q$ 是不含时间的算符.

由于 $H$ 与时间无关，所以

$$
U \left(t\right) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} H t / \hbar}, U ^ {\dagger} \left(t\right) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} H t / \hbar},
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} U (t)}{\mathrm {d} t} = \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} H\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} H t / \hbar} = - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} H U (t),
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} U ^ {\dagger} (t)}{\mathrm {d} t} = \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} H U (t) \right] ^ {\dagger} = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} U ^ {\dagger} (t) H.
$$

$H$ 不依赖时间，所以 $H$ 与 $U ^ { \dagger }$ 和 $U$ 都对易.困此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} Q _ {H} (t) = \frac {\mathrm {d} U ^ {\dagger} (t)}{\mathrm {d} t} Q U (t) + U ^ {\dagger} (t) Q \frac {\mathrm {d} U (t)}{\mathrm {d} t} \\ = \left(\frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} U ^ {\dagger} H\right) Q U + U ^ {\dagger} Q \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar}\right) H U \\ = - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(- U ^ {\dagger} H Q U + U ^ {\dagger} Q H U\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

ep

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} Q _ {H} (t) = - U ^ {\dagger} H Q U + U ^ {\dagger} Q H U,
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} Q _ {H} (t) = U ^ {\dagger} Q U H - H U ^ {+} Q U,
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} Q _ {H} (t) = Q _ {H} H - H Q _ {H} = [ Q _ {H}, H ].
$$

对于海森伯绘景下的算符 $\boldsymbol { x } _ { H }$ ，由 $\frac { \mathrm { d } } { \mathrm { d } t } Q _ { H } \left( t \right) = \frac { 1 } { \mathrm { i } \hbar } \left[ Q _ { H } , H \right]$ 知

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} x _ {H} = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} [ x _ {H}, H ] = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} \left[ U ^ {\dagger} x U, H \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} \left(U ^ {\dagger} [ x U, H ] + \left[ U ^ {\dagger}, H \right] x U\right) \\ = \frac {1}{i \hbar} \left(U ^ {\dagger} x [ U, H ] + U ^ {\dagger} [ x, H ] U + \left[ U ^ {\dagger}, H \right] x U\right) \\ = \frac {1}{i \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} [ x, H ] U \\ = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} \left[ x, \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + V (x) \right] U \\ = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} \left(\left[ x, \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \right] + [ x, V (x) ]\right) U \\ = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} \left(\left[ x, \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \right]\right) U \\ = \frac {1}{2 m i \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} (p [ x, p ] + [ x, p ] p) U \\ = \frac {1}{2 m i \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} (i \hbar) 2 p U \\ = \frac {p _ {H}}{m}. \\ \end{array}
$$

对于海森伯绘景下的算符 $p _ { H }$ 由: $\frac { \mathrm { d } } { \mathrm { d } t } Q _ { H } \left( t \right) = \frac { 1 } { \mathrm { i } \hbar } \left[ Q _ { H } , H \right]$ 结舍 $x _ { H }$ 的部分推导，直接可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} p _ {H} = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} \left[ p, \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + V \right] U \\ = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} \left(\left[ p, \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \right] + [ p, V ]\right) U \\ = \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \hbar} U ^ {\dagger} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} V}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) _ {H}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，海森伯绘景下算符 $x _ { H }$ 和 $p _ { H }$ 都存在经典对应关系:

$$
m \frac {\mathrm {d} x _ {H}}{\mathrm {d} t} = p _ {H} (t), \quad \frac {\mathrm {d} p _ {H}}{\mathrm {d} t} = \left(- \frac {\mathrm {d} V}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) _ {H}.
$$

牢牢牢习题 6.30 粒子做一维运动时的不舍时哈密顿量处在能量为 $E _ { n }$ 的定态 $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ .

(8) 证明:舍时薛定海方程的解可以写成

$$
\varPsi (x, t) = \hat {U} (t) \varPsi (x, 0) = \int K (x, x ^ {\prime}, t) \varPsi (x ^ {\prime}, 0) \mathrm {d} x ^ {\prime},
$$

其中 $K \left( x , x ^ { \prime } , t \right)$ 是熟知的传播子，即

$$
K \left(x, x ^ {\prime}, t\right) = \sum_ {n} \psi_ {n} ^ {*} \left(x ^ {\prime}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \psi_ {n} \left(x\right).
$$

其中 $\left| K \left( x , x ^ { \prime } , t \right) \right| ^ { 2 }$ 是量子力学粒子在 $t$ 时间内从 $x ^ { \prime }$ 位置传播到 $x$ 位置的儿率.

(b) 对质量为 $m$ 的粒子在频率为 $\omega$ 的简谐势场中的情况，求 $K$ 值.利用如下恒等式:

$$
\frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - z ^ {2}}} \exp \left[ - \frac {\xi^ {2} + \eta^ {2} - 2 \xi \eta z}{1 - z ^ {2}} \right] = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \eta^ {2}} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {z ^ {n}}{2 ^ {n} n !} H _ {n} (\xi) H _ {n} (\eta).
$$

(c) 如果 (a) 部分粒子的初恋为①

$$
\varPsi (x, 0) = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a (x - x _ {0}) ^ {2}}.
$$

求 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ .将结果与习题 .4组 作对比比.注释 习题 .4组 $a = m \omega / 2 \hbar$ 且的一个特例.

求质量为 $m$ 的自由粒子的 $K$ 值.在该情况下，定态不再是离散的，而是连续的，需要对方程 (6.79) 作如下替换:

$$
\sum_ {n} \rightarrow \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {d} p.
$$

(e) 求自由粒子以初态

$$
\varPsi (x, 0) = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x ^ {2}}
$$

出发的波函数审 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ ).将结果与习题 2.21 作对比.

解答 (a) 将零时刻的波函数目 $\varPsi \left( x , 0 \right)$ 用本征态进行展开

$$
\varPsi (x, 0) = \sum_ {n} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} (x),
$$

其中系数c. $\begin{array} { r } { c _ { n } = \int \psi _ { n } ^ { * } \left( x \right) \psi \left( x , 0 \right) \mathrm { d } x } \end{array}$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi (x, t) = \sum_ {n} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \\ = \sum_ {n} \left[ \int \psi_ {n} ^ {*} (x ^ {\prime}) \psi (x ^ {\prime}, 0) d x ^ {\prime} \right] \psi_ {n} (x) e ^ {- i E _ {n} t / \hbar} \\ = \int \sum_ {n} \psi_ {n} ^ {*} (x ^ {\prime}) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \psi_ {n} (x) \psi (x ^ {\prime}, 0) \mathrm {d} x ^ {\prime} \\ = \int K (x, x ^ {\prime}, t) \psi (x ^ {\prime}, 0) d x ^ {\prime}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\begin{array} { r } { K \left( x , x ^ { \prime } , t \right) = \underset { n } { \sum } \psi _ { n } ^ { * } \left( x ^ { \prime } \right) \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } E _ { n } t / \hbar } \psi _ { n } \left( x \right) . } \end{array}$ n

(b) 简谐振子的前为 $\psi _ { n } = A _ { n } H _ { n } ( \xi ) \mathrm { e } ^ { - \xi ^ { 2 } / 2 }$ 其中 $\xi = \sqrt { \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } x$

(8) 中传播子的形式可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} K \left(x, x ^ {\prime}, t\right) = \sum_ {n} \psi_ {n} ^ {*} \left(x ^ {\prime}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \psi_ {n} (x) \\ = \sum_ {n = 0} A _ {n} H _ {n} (\eta) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \eta^ {2} / 2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (n + 1 / 2) \omega t} A _ {n} H _ {n} (\xi) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 η= $\eta = \sqrt { \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } x ^ { \prime }$ $z = \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } \omega t }$ 由方程 (2.86) ${ A _ { n } } = \left( { m \omega / \pi \hbar } \right) ^ { 1 / 4 } \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { 2 ^ { n } n ! } }$

因此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} K \left(x, x ^ {\prime}, t\right) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\left(\eta^ {2} + \xi^ {2}\right) / 2} \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \sqrt {z} \mathrm {e} ^ {\eta^ {2} + \xi^ {2}} \sum_ {n = 0} \frac {z ^ {n}}{2 ^ {n} n !} H _ {n} (\xi) H _ {n} (\eta) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {z}{1 - z ^ {2}}} \exp \left[ \frac {\eta^ {2} + \xi^ {2}}{2} - \frac {\eta^ {2} + \xi^ {2} - 2 \eta \xi z}{1 - z ^ {2}} \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {z}{1 - z ^ {2}}} \exp \left[ - \frac {1}{2} \frac {(1 + z ^ {2}) (\eta^ {2} + \xi^ {2}) - 4 \eta \xi z}{1 - z ^ {2}} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

由于 $| z | ^ { 2 } = z ^ { * } z = 1 .$ ，所以

$$
K \left(x, x ^ {\prime}, t\right) = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {1}{z ^ {*} - z}} \exp \left[ - \frac {1}{2} \frac {\left(z ^ {*} + z\right) \left(\eta^ {2} + \xi^ {2}\right) - 4 \eta \xi}{z ^ {*} - z} \right].
$$

z. - z = e!wt - e-Iωt = 2isin(叫， $z ^ { * } + z = \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } \omega t } + \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } \omega t } = 2 \cos ( \omega t )$ 因此上式可进一步写为

$$
K \left(x, x ^ {\prime}, t\right) = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{2 \pi \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t)}} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \frac {\cos (\omega t) \left(x ^ {2} + x ^ {\prime 2}\right) - 2 x x ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t)} \right].
$$

初始态为审 $\varPsi \left( x ^ { \prime } , 0 \right) = \left( \frac { 2 a } { \pi } \right) ^ { 1 / 4 } \mathrm { e } ^ { - a \left( x ^ { \prime } - x _ { 0 } \right) ^ { 2 } } .$

随时阿演化态可以写为

$$
\varPsi (x, t) = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} K (x, x ^ {\prime}, t) \varPsi (x ^ {\prime}, 0) \mathrm {d} x ^ {\prime}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \sqrt {\frac {1}{\pi} \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t)}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \frac {\cos (\omega t) (x ^ {2} + x ^ {\prime 2}) - 2 x x ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t)} \right] \\ \times \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a \left(x ^ {\prime} - x _ {0}\right) ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x ^ {\prime} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {1}{\pi} \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t)}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \frac {x ^ {2}}{\mathrm {i} \tan (\omega t)} - a x _ {0} ^ {2} \right] \\ \times \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \exp \left\{- \left[ \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \tan (\omega t)} + a \right] x ^ {\prime 2} + \left[ \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \frac {x}{\mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t)} + 2 a x _ {0} \right] x ^ {\prime} \right\} \mathrm {d} x ^ {\prime}. \\ \end{array}
$$

中括号内的第一项积分为高斯积分形式，第二项积分为普通的 指数积分，因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi (x, t) = \sqrt {\frac {1}{\pi} \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t)}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \frac {x ^ {2}}{\mathrm {i} \tan (\omega t)} - a x _ {0} ^ {2} \right] \\ \times \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{\frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \tan (\omega t)} + a}} \exp \left\{\frac {\left[ \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \frac {x}{\mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t)} + 2 a x _ {0} \right] ^ {2}}{4 \left[ \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \frac {1}{\mathrm {i} \tan (\omega t)} + a \right]} \right\}. \\ \end{array}
$$

与习题 .4 进行对比，设 $t = 0$ ，则

$$
\varPsi (x,0) = \left(\frac{m\omega}{\pi\hbar}\right)^{1 / 4}\exp \left[ - \frac{m\omega}{2\hbar} (x - x_{0})^{2}\right],
$$

令 $a = \frac { m \omega } { 2 \hbar }$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Psi \left(x, t\right) = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {\exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \frac {\left(x ^ {2} + x _ {0} ^ {2}\right) \cos (\omega t) - 2 x x _ {0} + \mathrm {i} \sin (\omega t) x ^ {2}}{\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t}} \right]}{\sqrt {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t}}} \\ = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left\{- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left[ x ^ {2} + \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} \left(1 + \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) + \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar t}{m} - 2 \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} x x _ {0} \right] \right\}. \\ \end{array}
$$

结论与习题 一致.

(d) 对于自由粒子，将分离形式的传播子 $\boldsymbol { K } \left( \boldsymbol { x } , \boldsymbol { x } ^ { \prime } , t \right) = \sum _ { n } \boldsymbol { \psi } _ { n } ^ { * } \left( \boldsymbol { x } ^ { \prime } \right) \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } E _ { n } t / \hbar } \boldsymbol { \psi } _ { n } \left( \boldsymbol { x } \right)$ 换为积分形式，

$$
\begin{array}{l} K \left(x, x ^ {\prime}, t\right) = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} f _ {p} \left(x ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {*} e ^ {- i E _ {p} t / \hbar} f _ {p} (x) d p \\ = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x ^ {\prime} / \hbar}}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p ^ {2} t / 2 m \hbar} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar}}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \mathrm {d} p \\ = \frac {1}{2 \pi \hbar} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {p ^ {2} t}{2 m \hbar} + \mathrm {i} \frac {p}{\hbar} (x - x ^ {\prime}) \right] \mathrm {d} p \\ = \frac {1}{2 \pi \hbar} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{\mathrm {i} t / 2 m \hbar}} \exp \left[ - \frac {\left(x - x ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {2} / \hbar^ {2}}{2 \mathrm {i} t / (m \hbar)} \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m}{2 \pi \mathrm {i} \hbar t}} \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{2 \hbar t} \left(x - x ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {2} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

(e) 披函数

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi (x, t) = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} K (x, x ^ {\prime}, t) \varPsi (x ^ {\prime}, 0) d x ^ {\prime} \\ = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \sqrt {\frac {m}{2 \pi \mathrm {i} \hbar t}} \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{2 \hbar t} \left(x - x ^ {\prime}\right) ^ {2} \right] \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x ^ {\prime 2}} \mathrm {d} x ^ {\prime} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m}{2 \pi \mathrm {i} \hbar t}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{2 \hbar t} x ^ {2} \right] \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \exp \left[ - \left(a - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{2 \hbar t}\right) x ^ {\prime 2} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{\hbar t} x x ^ {\prime} \right] \mathrm {d} x ^ {\prime} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m}{2 \pi \mathrm {i} \hbar t}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} m}{2 \hbar t} x ^ {2} \right] \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{a - \mathrm {i} m / (2 \hbar t)}} \exp \left[ - \frac {[ m x / (\hbar t) ] ^ {2}}{4 [ a - \mathrm {i} m / (2 \hbar t) ]} \right] \\ = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {\exp \left[ - \frac {a}{1 + \mathrm {i} 2 a \hbar t / m} x ^ {2} \right]}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} 2 a \hbar t / m}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

结论与习题 2.21 一致.

# 补充苟题

习题 在推导方程 时，假定函数具有泰勒级数形式如果通过谱分解定义算符指数而不是罪级数，那么结果更具有普适性，

$$
\hat {T} (a) = \int \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / \hbar} | p \rangle \langle p | \mathrm {d} p.
$$

这里给出狄拉克符号下的算符;作用在位置空间函数(参见教材 123 页的讨论)意味着

$$
\hat {T} (a) \psi (x) = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / \hbar} f _ {p} (x) \Phi (p) \mathrm {d} p,
$$

其中 $\varPhi \left( p \right)$ 是对应 $\psi \left( x \right)$ )的动量空间波函数 $f _ { p } \left( x \right)$ )在方程(何3.32功)中已给出定义.证明:方程 (6.81) 给出的算符 ${ \hat { T } } \left( a \right)$ 作用到函数

$$
\psi (x) = \sqrt {\lambda} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda | x |}
$$

(其一阶导数在 $x = 0$ 处未定义)上可以得到正确的结果.

证明 首先通过傅里叶变换将被函数 $\psi \left( x \right) = \sqrt { \lambda } \mathrm { e } ^ { - \lambda | x | }$ 变换到动量空间上

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Phi (p) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \psi (x) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \sqrt {\lambda} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda | x |} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\sqrt {\lambda}}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda | x | - \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \hbar}} \left[ \int_ {- \infty} ^ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {(\lambda - \mathrm {i} p / \hbar) x} \mathrm {d} x + \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (\lambda + \mathrm {i} p / \hbar) x} \mathrm {d} x \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \hbar}} \left(\frac {1}{\lambda - \mathrm {i} p / \hbar} + \frac {1}{\lambda + \mathrm {i} p / \hbar}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
= \sqrt {\frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \hbar}} \frac {\lambda}{(p / \hbar) ^ {2} + \lambda^ {2}}.
$$

再利用方程 (6.81) ，得

$$
\hat {T} (a) \psi (x) = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p a / \hbar} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar}}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {2 \lambda}{\pi \hbar}} \frac {\lambda}{(p / \hbar) ^ {2} + \lambda^ {2}} \mathrm {d} p.
$$

为方便积分，令 $p = q \lambda \hbar$ ，得

$$
\hat {T} (a) \psi (x) = \frac {\sqrt {\lambda}}{\pi} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} q \lambda (x - a)}}{q ^ {2} + 1} \mathrm {d} q = \frac {2 \sqrt {\lambda^ {3}}}{\pi} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\cos [ q \lambda (x - a) ]}{q ^ {2} + 1} \mathrm {d} q = \sqrt {\lambda} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda | x - a |},
$$

与所求结果符合.

事*习题 6.32 自旋态的旋转可由与方程 (6.32) 相同的表达式给出，其中用自旋角动量代替轨道角动量:

$$
\mathcal {R} _ {\boldsymbol {n}} (\varphi) = \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} \right].
$$

本题将考虑自旋为 1/2 态的旋转.

(a) 证明:

$$
\left(\boldsymbol {a} \cdot \sigma\right) \left(\boldsymbol {b} \cdot \sigma\right) = \boldsymbol {a} \cdot \boldsymbol {b} + \mathrm {i} \left(\boldsymbol {a} \times \boldsymbol {b}\right) \cdot \sigma ,
$$

其中 $\sigma _ { i }$ 是泡利自旋矩阵， $^ { a }$ 和 $^ { b }$ 为普通的矢量.利用习题 4.29 的结果.

(b) 利用 (a) 部分的结果，证明:

$$
\exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {S} \right] = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \sigma .
$$

回顾一下 $\pmb { S } = \left( \hbar / 2 \right) \mathbf { \sigma }$

(c) 证明:在沿着 $z$ 轴自旋向上和自旋向下的标准基下， (b) 部分的结果变为如下矩阵

$$
\mathcal {R} _ {n} = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{l l} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \theta & \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos \theta \end{array} \right),
$$

其中 $\theta$ 和 $\phi$ 是描述旋转轴单位矢量" $\mathbf { \nabla } _ { \mathbf { \eta } ^ { \prime } }$ 极坐标.

(d) 证明: (c) 部分的矩阵 $\textstyle { \mathcal { R } } _ { n }$ 是么正的.   
(e) 详尽地计算矩阵 ${ \pmb S } _ { x } ^ { \prime } = \pmb { \mathcal { R } } ^ { \dagger } { \pmb S } _ { x } \pmb { \mathcal { R } }$ ，其中 $\mathbfcal { R }$ 表示绕 $z$ 轴旋转角度 $\varphi$ 并验证其是否回到预期的结果.提示用 $s _ { x }$ 和 $\mathcal { S } _ { y }$ 重写 $\pmb { S } _ { x } ^ { \prime }$   
(f)构建一个绕 $x$ 轴旋转角度为 $\pi$ 的矩阵，证明:它可以把自旋向上变为自旋向下.  
(g) 求出绕 $z$ 轴旋转 $2 \pi$ 矩阵.为什么这个结果令人吃惊?①

解答 (a)

$$
\left(\boldsymbol {a} \cdot \sigma\right) \left(\boldsymbol {b} \cdot \sigma\right) = \sum_ {j} a _ {j} \sigma_ {j} \sum_ {k} b _ {k} \sigma_ {k} = \sum_ {j} \sum_ {k} a _ {j} b _ {k} \sigma_ {j} \sigma_ {k}.
$$

由习题 4.29 知 $\sigma _ { j } \sigma _ { k } = \delta _ { j k } + \mathrm { i } \sum _ { l } \varepsilon _ { j k l } \sigma _ { l }$ ，代入上式，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\boldsymbol {a} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma}\right) \left(\boldsymbol {b} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma}\right) = \sum_ {j} \sum_ {k} a _ {j} b _ {k} \left(\delta_ {j k} + \mathrm {i} \sum_ {n} \varepsilon_ {j k n} \sigma_ {n}\right) \\ = \sum_ {j} \sum_ {k} a _ {j} b _ {k} \delta_ {j k} + \mathrm {i} \sum_ {j} \sum_ {k} a _ {j} b _ {k} \left(\sum_ {n} \varepsilon_ {j k n} \sigma_ {n}\right) \\ = \sum_ {j} a _ {j} b _ {j} + \mathrm {i} \sum_ {n} \sum_ {j} \sum_ {k} \varepsilon_ {j k n} a _ {j} b _ {k} \sigma_ {n} \\ = \boldsymbol {a} \cdot \boldsymbol {b} + \mathrm {i} \sum_ {n} (\boldsymbol {a} \times \boldsymbol {b}) _ {n} \sigma_ {n} \\ = \boldsymbol {a} \cdot \boldsymbol {b} + \mathrm {i} (\boldsymbol {a} \times \boldsymbol {b}) \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 由于表达式中包含 $\pmb { n } \cdot \pmb { S } = \frac { \hbar } { 2 } \pmb { n } \cdot \pmb { \sigma }$ 所以先研究 $( n \cdot \sigma ) ^ { k }$ 的基本结果

$$
\left(\boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma}\right) ^ {2} = \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {n} + \mathrm {i} (\boldsymbol {n} \times \boldsymbol {n}) \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma} = \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {n} = 1.
$$

类推可知

$$
\left(\pmb {n} \cdot \pmb {\sigma}\right) ^ {k} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {\pmb {n} \cdot \pmb {\sigma},} & {k \text {为 奇 数},} \\ {1,} & {k \text {为 偶 数}.} \end{array} \right.
$$

将以上性质应用到要证明的式子中，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} \right] = \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \left(\frac {\hbar}{2} \boldsymbol {\sigma}\right) \right] \\ = \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{2} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma} \right] \\ = \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{k !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} \varphi}{2}\right) ^ {k} (\boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma}) ^ {k} \\ = \sum_ {k e v e n} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{k !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} \varphi}{2}\right) ^ {k} + \sum_ {k o d d} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{k !} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} \varphi}{2}\right) ^ {k} (\boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma}) \\ = \sum_ {k e v e n} ^ {\infty} \frac {(- 1) ^ {k / 2}}{k !} \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) ^ {k} - \mathrm {i} \sum_ {k o d d} ^ {\infty} \frac {(- 1) ^ {(k - 1) / 2}}{k !} \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) ^ {k} (\boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma}) \\ = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(c)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {R} _ {n} (\varphi) = \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{\hbar} \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {S} \right] \\ = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \boldsymbol {n} \cdot \boldsymbol {\sigma} \\ = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left(n _ {x} \sigma_ {x} + n _ {y} \sigma_ {y} + n _ {z} \sigma_ {z}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

由于 $\pmb { n } = \sin \theta \cos \phi \hat { \bf i } + \sin \theta \sin \phi \hat { j } + \cos \theta \hat { k }$ ，代入上式，得

$$
\mathcal {R} _ {n} (\varphi) = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left[ \sin \theta \cos \phi \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right) \right.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. + \sin \theta \sin \phi \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & - \mathrm {i} \\ \mathrm {i} & 0 \end{array} \right) + \cos \theta \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & - 1 \end{array} \right) \right] \\ = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{l l} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \theta & \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos \theta \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

(d)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {R}} _ {n} ^ {+} = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) ^ {\mathrm {T}} + \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \theta & \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos \theta \end{array} \right) ^ {\mathrm {T}} \\ = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) + \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \theta & \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos \theta \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

很明显， $\mathcal { R } _ { n } ^ { \dagger }$ 是一个绕 $^ { n }$ 轴旋转 $- \varphi / 2$ 的操作.

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {R} _ {n} \mathcal {R} _ {n} ^ {\dagger} = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) \\ + \mathrm {i} \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \theta & \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos \theta \end{array} \right) \\ + \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \theta & \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos \theta \end{array} \right) \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) \\ + \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \theta & \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos \theta \end{array} \right) \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \theta & \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin \theta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos \theta \end{array} \right) \\ = \cos^ {2} \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) + \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos^ {2} \theta + \sin^ {2} \theta & 0 \\ 0 & \sin^ {2} \theta + \cos^ {2} \theta \end{array} \right) \\ = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

(e) 沿着 $z$ 轴旋转时， $\theta = 0$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {R} _ {z} = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos 0 & \sin 0 \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} \\ \sin 0 \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi} & - \cos 0 \end{array} \right) \\ = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) \\ = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) & 0 \\ 0 & \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) + \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} _ {x} ^ {\prime} = \boldsymbol {\mathcal {R}} ^ {\dagger} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} _ {x} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {R}} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) + \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) & 0 \\ 0 & \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \end{array} \right) \frac {\hbar}{2} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right) \\ \times \left( \begin{array}{c c} \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) & 0 \\ 0 & \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) + \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \end{array} \right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {\hbar}{2} \left[ \cos \varphi \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right) - \sin \varphi \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & - \mathrm {i} \\ \mathrm {i} & 0 \end{array} \right) \right] \\ = \boldsymbol {S} _ {x} \cos \varphi - \boldsymbol {S} _ {y} \sin \varphi . \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {R}} _ {x} (\phi) = \exp \left(- \mathrm {i} \frac {\varphi}{\hbar} n _ {x} \frac {\hbar}{2} \sigma_ {x}\right) \\ = \cos \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\varphi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

当 $\varphi = \pi$ 时，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {R} _ {x} \left(\pi\right) = \cos \left(\frac {\pi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {\pi}{2}\right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right) \\ = - \mathrm {i} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

${ \mathcal { R } } _ { x } \left( \pi \right)$ 作用时叫态 $\left( \begin{array} { l } { 1 } \\ { 0 } \end{array} \right)$

$$
\mathcal {R} _ {x} \left(\pi\right) \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right) = - \mathrm {i} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right) = - \mathrm {i} \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \end{array} \right).
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {R} _ {z} (2 \pi) = \cos \left(\frac {2 \pi}{2}\right) - \mathrm {i} \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi}{2}\right) \sigma_ {z} \\ = \cos (\pi) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) - \mathrm {i} \sin (\pi) \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & - 1 \end{array} \right) \\ = - 1 \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) \\ = \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \pi} \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

绕 $z$ 轴旋转一个周期后,多出一个相位因子 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } \pi }$

串串串己-ó习题 6.33 考虑处于边长为 $L$ 的二维无限深方势阱中质量为 $m$ 的粒子原点作为势阱的中心，定态可以写为

$$
\psi_ {n _ {x} n _ {y}} (x, y) = \frac {2}{L} \sin \left[ \frac {n _ {x} \pi}{L} \left(x - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \sin \left[ \frac {n _ {y} \pi}{L} \left(y - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right],
$$

能量为

$$
E _ {n _ {x} n _ {y}} = \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m L ^ {2}} \left(n _ {x} ^ {2} + n _ {y} ^ {2}\right),
$$

其中 $n _ { x }$ 和 $n _ { y }$ 为正整数.

(a) $a \neq b$ 的两个态 $\psi _ { a b }$ 和 $\psi _ { b a }$ 明显是简井的.证明:围绕势阱中心逆时针旋转$9 0 ^ { \circ }$ 可以使一个态转变为另一个态

$$
\hat {R} \psi_ {a b} \propto \psi_ {b a},
$$

并确定比例常数.提示把 $\psi _ { a b }$ 写成极坐标形式.

(b) 假定用两个基矢 $\psi _ { + }$ 和 $\psi _ { - }$ 作为简并态来替代 $\psi _ { a b }$ 和 $\psi _ { b a }$

$$
\psi_ {\pm} = \frac {\psi_ {a b} \pm \psi_ {b a}}{\sqrt {2}}.
$$

证明:如果 $a$ 和 $b$ 都是偶数或都是奇数， $\psi _ { + } ^ { \phantom { \dagger } }$ 和 $\psi _ { - }$ 是转动算符的本征态.

对于 $a = 5$ 和 $b = 7$ ，绘制 $\psi _ { - }$ 态的等高线图，并(目视)验证它是正方形每个对称操作的本征态(旋转 $\pi / 2$ 的整数倍、沿对角线上的反射或沿两边中线的反射).事实上， $\psi _ { + }$ 和 $\psi _ { - }$ 并没有同正方形的任何对称性操作联系起来，这意味着必须存在额外的对称性来解释这两个态的简并度.①

解答刨根据提示，先将波函数写成极坐标的形式

$$
x = r \cos \phi , y = r \sin \phi .
$$

$$
\psi_ {a, b} (r, \phi) = \frac {2}{L} \sin \left[ \frac {a \pi}{L} \left(r \cos \phi - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \sin \left[ \frac {b \pi}{L} \left(r \sin \phi - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right].
$$

沿着中心轴逆时针旋转 $9 0 ^ { \circ }$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} R _ {z} \left(\frac {\pi}{2}\right) \psi_ {a, b} (r, \phi) = \frac {2}{L} \sin \left\{\frac {a \pi}{L} \left[ r \cos \left(\phi - \frac {\pi}{2}\right) - \frac {L}{2} \right] \right\} \sin \left\{\frac {b \pi}{L} \left[ r \sin \left(\phi - \frac {\pi}{2}\right) - \frac {L}{2} \right] \right\} \\ = \frac {2}{L} \sin \left\{\frac {a \pi}{L} \left[ r \sin \phi - \frac {L}{2} \right] \right\} \sin \left\{\frac {b \pi}{L} \left[ - r \cos \phi - \frac {L}{2} \right] \right\} \\ = \frac {2}{L} \sin \left[ \frac {a \pi}{L} \left(y - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \sin \left[ - \frac {b \pi}{L} \left(x + \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {2}{L} \sin \left[ \frac {b \pi}{L} \left(x - \frac {L}{2}\right) + b \pi \right] \sin \left[ \frac {a \pi}{L} \left(y - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {2}{L} \left\{\sin \left[ \frac {b \pi}{L} \left(x - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \cos (b \pi) + \cos \left[ \frac {b \pi}{L} \left(x - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \sin (b \pi) \right\} \\ \times \sin \left[ \frac {a \pi}{L} \left(y - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \\ = (- 1) ^ {b + 1} \frac {2}{L} \sin \left[ \frac {b \pi}{L} \left(x - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \sin \left[ \frac {a \pi}{L} \left(y - \frac {L}{2}\right) \right] \\ = (- 1) ^ {b + 1} \psi_ {b, a} (r, \phi). \\ \end{array}
$$

(b)利用(a)中的结论,

$$
\begin{array}{l} R \psi_ {\pm} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ R \psi_ {a b} \pm R \psi_ {b a} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ (- 1) ^ {b + 1} \psi_ {b a} \pm (- 1) ^ {a + 1} \psi_ {a b} \right] \\ = (- 1) ^ {a + 1} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ (- 1) ^ {b - a} \psi_ {b a} \pm \psi_ {a b} \right] \\ = \pm (- 1) ^ {a + 1} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {a b} \pm (- 1) ^ {b - a} \psi_ {b a} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

如果 $a$ 和 $b$ 同时为奇数或者同时为偶数，那么 $( - 1 ) ^ { a - b } = 1$ . 上式为

$$
R \psi_ {\pm} = \pm (- 1) ^ {a + 1} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} [ \psi_ {a b} \pm \psi_ {b a} ] = \pm (- 1) ^ {a + 1} \psi_ {\pm}.
$$

即 $\psi _ { + }$ 和 $\psi _ { - }$ 是转动算符的本征态，本征值为 $\pm ( - 1 ) ^ { a + 1 }$

) $a = 5$ 和 $b = 7$ 时，

$$
\psi_ {-} = \frac {\psi_ {5 7} - \psi_ {7 5}}{\sqrt {2}}.
$$

athematic 程序如下

```autohotkey
L = 1;  
Psi[a_, b_, x_, y_]:= 2/L Sin[a Pi/L(x-L/2)] Sin[b Pi/L(y-L/2)];  
Psineg[x_, y_]:= (Psi[5, 7, x, y] - Psi[7, 5, x, y]) / Squr[2];  
Psipos[x_, y_]:= (Psi[5, 7, x, y] + Psi[7, 5, x, y]) / Squr[2];  
ContourPlot[Psineg[x, y], {x, -L/2, L/2}, {y, -L/2, L/2}]  
ContourPlot[Psipos[x, y], {x, -L/2, L/2}, {y, -L/2, L/2}] 
```

$\psi _ { - }$ 态的等高线图见图 6.2

![](images/2a8f77b067a5de6d42cf6587400e0050a2659778dd6713b490f11db3875fe9bd.jpg)  
6.2

这两个态的能量简并度并不能通过正方形的对称性联系起来，它们的简并关系请查阅偶然简并(F. Leyvraz, et a l. ,Am. J. Phys. 65, 1087 (1997)) .

牢牢牢习题 34 相比简单的旋转不变性 库仑势有更高的对称性.这种额外的对称性表现为额外的守恒量 即拉普拉斯一朗格一楞次矢量

$$
\hat {\boldsymbol {M}} = \frac {\hat {\boldsymbol {p}} \times \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} - \hat {\boldsymbol {L}} \times \hat {\boldsymbol {p}}}{2 m} + V (\boldsymbol {r}) \hat {\boldsymbol {r}},
$$

其中 $V \left( r \right)$ 是势能 $V ( r ) = - \mathrm { e } ^ { 2 } / ( 4 \pi \varepsilon _ { 0 } r )$ .①氢原子守恒量对易式的完备集是

(i) $\left[ \hat { H } , \hat { M } _ { i } \right] = 0$   
(ii) $\left[ \hat { H } , \hat { L } _ { i } \right] = 0$   
ii) $\left[ \hat { L } _ { i } , \hat { L } _ { j } \right] = \mathrm { i } \hbar \varepsilon _ { i j k } \hat { L } _ { k }$

(iv) $\left[ \hat { L } _ { i } , \hat { M } _ { j } \right] = \mathrm { i } \hbar \varepsilon _ { i j k } \hat { M } _ { k }$   
(v) $\left[ \hat { M } _ { i } , \ \hat { M } _ { j } \right] = \frac { \hbar } { \mathrm { i } } \varepsilon _ { i j k } L _ { k } \frac { 2 } { m } \hat { H } .$

这些量的物理含义为: (i) $M$ 是守恒量， (ii) $\pmb { L }$ 是守恒量， (iii) $\pmb { L }$ 是矢量， (iv) $M$ 是矢量 ((v) 没有明确的解释).矢量 $\hat { L }$ 和 $\hat { M }$ 与 $\hat { H }$ 还有两个附加关系，它们是

${ \hat { M } } ^ { 2 } = \left( { \frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { 2 } } { 4 \pi \varepsilon _ { 0 } } } \right) ^ { 2 } + { \frac { 2 } { m } } { \hat { H } } \left( { \hat { L } } ^ { 2 } + { \hbar } ^ { 2 } \right)$   
(vii) $\hat { M } \cdot \hat { L } = 0 .$

(a) 从习题 6.19 中的结果可知 $\hat { M }$ 是守恒量，对于某些常数 $c _ { n \ell }$ 有 ${ \hat { M } } _ { + } \psi _ { n \ell \ell } =$ $c _ { n \ell } \psi _ { n ( \ell + 1 ) ( \ell + 1 ) }$ .将(世 式作用到 $\psi _ { n \ell \ell }$ 态上，证明:

$$
\hat {M} _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {\ell + 1}} c _ {n \ell} \psi_ {n (\ell + 1) \ell}.
$$

(b) 利用 (vi) 证明:

$$
\hat {M} _ {-} \hat {M} _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left[ 1 - \left(\frac {\ell + 1}{n}\right) ^ {2} \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell} - \hat {M} _ {z} ^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}.
$$

(c) (a) (b) 部分的结果求出常数 $c _ { n \ell }$ 你会发现除非 $\ell = n - 1$ 否则 $c _ { n \ell }$ 是非萃的提示 考虑 $\textstyle \int { \big | } M _ { + } \psi _ { n \ell m } { \big | } ^ { 2 } \mathrm { d } ^ { 3 } r$ 并结合 $M _ { \pm }$ 互为厄米共辄这一事实.图 6.3展示了 $\hat { L }$ 和 $\hat { M }$ 的生成元是如何关联氧原子简并态的

![](images/fdd165bab205a735b4475f4f8350a6ce8a12448d2539a0e19681c4a6c68ff841.jpg)  
6.3 氢原子 $n = 3$ 的筒并态，以及与它们相关联的对称操作

解答 (a) 根据提示，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {M} \cdot \hat {L} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left(M _ {x} L _ {x} + M _ {y} L _ {y} + M _ {z} L _ {z}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \\ = \left(\frac {M _ {+} + M _ {-}}{2} \frac {L _ {+} + L _ {-}}{2} + \frac {M _ {+} - M _ {-}}{2 \mathrm {i}} \frac {L _ {+} - L _ {-}}{2 \mathrm {i}} + M _ {z} L _ {z}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \\ = \left(\frac {M _ {+} L _ {-} + M _ {-} L _ {+}}{2} + M _ {z} L _ {z}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由 $\hat { M } _ { + } \psi _ { n \ell \ell } = c _ { n \ell } \psi _ { n ( \ell + 1 ) ( \ell + 1 ) }$ ，将上式的 $\hat { M } _ { + }$ 变换到与被函数直接作用，有助于结果的化简于是

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {M} \cdot \hat {L} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \frac {1}{2} \left(L _ {-} M _ {+} + [ M _ {+}, L _ {-} ]\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + \frac {1}{2} M _ {-} L _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {z} L _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \\ = \frac {1}{2} L _ {-} M _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + \frac {1}{2} \left[ M _ {+}, L _ {-} \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + 0 + l \hbar M _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \\ = \frac {1}{2} c _ {n \ell} L _ {-} \psi_ {n (\ell + 1) (\ell + 1)} - \frac {1}{2} \left[ L _ {-}, M _ {+} \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + \hbar l M _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}. \\ \end{array}
$$

再由方程 (6 $\left[ \hat { L } _ { - } , \hat { V } _ { + } \right] = - 2 \hbar \hat { V } _ { z }$ 和 $L _ { - } \psi _ { n \ell m } = \hbar \sqrt { \ell ( \ell + 1 ) - m ( m - 1 ) } \psi _ { n \ell ( m - 1 ) }$ 上式可继续化简为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {M} \cdot \hat {L} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \frac {1}{2} c _ {n \ell} B _ {\ell + 1} ^ {\ell} \psi_ {n (\ell + 1) \ell} - \frac {1}{2} (- 2 \hbar M _ {z}) \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + \hbar \ell M _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \\ = \frac {1}{2} \hbar \sqrt {l + 1} c _ {n \ell} \psi_ {n (\ell + 1) \ell} + (\ell + 1) \hbar M _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

化简后，得

$$
M _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {l + 1}} c _ {n \ell} \psi_ {n (\ell + 1) \ell}.
$$

(b) 因为

$$
M ^ {2} = M \cdot M = \frac {M _ {+} M _ {-} + M _ {-} M _ {+}}{2} + M _ {z} ^ {2}.
$$

结合

$$
\hat {M} ^ {2} = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {2}{m} \hat {H} \left(\hat {L} ^ {2} + \hbar^ {2}\right),
$$

得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {M _ {+} M _ {-} + M _ {-} M _ {+}}{2} + M _ {z} ^ {2}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left[ \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {2}{m} \hat {H} (\hat {L} ^ {2} + \hbar^ {2}) \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell}. \\ \left(\frac {1}{2} M _ {+} M _ {-} + \frac {1}{2} M _ {-} M _ {+} + M _ {z} ^ {2}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left\{\left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {2}{m} E _ {n} \left[ \ell (\ell + 1) \hbar^ {2} + \hbar^ {2} \right] \right\} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}, \\ \frac {1}{2} \left[ M _ {+}, M _ {-} \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {-} M _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {z} ^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \\ = \left\{\left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {2}{m} \left[ - \frac {m}{2 \hbar^ {2}} \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \right] [ \ell (\ell + 1) \hbar^ {2} + \hbar^ {2} ] \right\} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} \left[ M _ {+}, M _ {-} \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {-} M _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {z} ^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left(1 - \frac {\ell^ {2} + \ell + 1}{n ^ {2}}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell}.
$$

继续化筒，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} [ M _ {+}, M _ {-} ] = [ M _ {x}, M _ {y} ] + \mathrm {i} [ M _ {y}, M _ {x} ] - \mathrm {i} [ M _ {x}, M _ {y} ] - [ M _ {y}, M _ {y} ] \\ = - 2 \mathrm {i} [ M _ {x}, M _ {y} ] \\ = - \frac {4}{m} \hbar L _ {z} H. \\ \end{array}
$$

将化筒结果代入上式，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{2} \left(- \frac {4}{m} \hbar L _ {z} H\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {-} M _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {z} ^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left(1 - \frac {\ell^ {2} + \ell + 1}{n ^ {2}}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell}. \\ - \frac {2}{m} \hbar^ {2} \ell E _ {n} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {-} M _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} + M _ {z} ^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left(1 - \frac {\ell^ {2} + \ell + 1}{n ^ {2}}\right) \psi_ {n \ell \ell}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，得

$$
\hat {M} _ {-} \hat {M} _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left[ 1 - \left(\frac {\ell + 1}{n}\right) ^ {2} \right] \psi_ {n \ell \ell} - \hat {M} _ {z} ^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}.
$$

(c) 根据提示 $\begin{array} { r } { \int { | M _ { + } \psi _ { n \ell m } | ^ { 2 } } \mathrm { d } ^ { 3 } r } \end{array}$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| c _ {n \ell} \right| ^ {2} = \int \left(M _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right) ^ {*} \left(M _ {+} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r \\ = \int \psi_ {n \ell \ell} ^ {*} (M _ {-} M _ {+}) \psi_ {n \ell \ell} d ^ {3} r. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) tflH) $\hat { M } _ { - } \hat { M } _ { + } \psi _ { n \ell \ell } = \left( \frac { \mathrm { e } ^ { 2 } } { 4 \pi \varepsilon _ { 0 } } \right) ^ { 2 } \left[ 1 - \left( \frac { \ell + 1 } { n } \right) ^ { 2 } \right] \psi _ { n \ell \ell } - \hat { M } _ { z } ^ { 2 } \psi _ { n \ell \ell }$ 结论代入上式，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} | c _ {n \ell} | ^ {2} = \int \psi_ {n \ell \ell} ^ {*} \left\{\left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left[ 1 - \left(\frac {\ell + 1}{n}\right) ^ {2} \right] - M _ {z} ^ {2} \right\} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r \\ = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left[ 1 - \left(\frac {\ell + 1}{n}\right) ^ {2} \right] \int \psi_ {n \ell \ell} ^ {*} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r - \int \psi_ {n \ell \ell} ^ {*} M _ {z} ^ {2} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r \\ = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left[ 1 - \left(\frac {\ell + 1}{n}\right) ^ {2} \right] - \int \left(M _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell}\right) ^ {*} M _ {z} \psi_ {n \ell \ell} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r. \\ \end{array}
$$

(a) 部分 $M _ { z } \psi _ { n \ell \ell } = - \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { 2 } } \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { \ell + 1 } } c _ { n \ell } \psi _ { n ( \ell + 1 ) \ell }$ !代入上式，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} | c _ {n \ell} | ^ {2} = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left[ 1 - \left(\frac {\ell + 1}{n}\right) ^ {2} \right] - \frac {1}{2} \frac {1}{\ell + 1} | c _ {n \ell} | ^ {2} \int \psi_ {n (\ell + 1) \ell} ^ {*} \psi_ {n (\ell + 1) \ell} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r \\ = \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left[ 1 - \left(\frac {\ell + 1}{n}\right) ^ {2} \right] - \frac {1}{2} \frac {1}{\ell + 1} | c _ {n \ell} | ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，得

$$
| c _ {n \ell} | ^ {2} = \frac {2 \ell + 3}{2 \ell + 2} \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \left[ 1 - \left(\frac {\ell + 1}{n}\right) ^ {2} \right].
$$

串串习题 6.35 伽利略变换是将参考系 $\boldsymbol { S }$ 变换到相对 $\boldsymbol { S }$ 以速度 $- v$ 运动的参考系$S ^ { \prime }$ 的操作(两个参考系在 $t = 0$ 时刻原点重合).在 $t$ 时刻伽利略变换的东正算符为

$$
\hat {\Gamma} (v, t) = \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} v (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \right].
$$

(a) 求无限小速度 $\delta$ 变换下i; $\hat { x } ^ { \prime } = \hat { \Gamma } ^ { \dagger } \hat { x } \hat { \Gamma }$ 和异 $\hat { p } ^ { \prime } = \hat { \Gamma } ^ { \dagger } \hat { p } \hat { \Gamma }$ '.你所得结果的物理意义是什么?

(b) 证明:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {\Gamma} (v, t) = \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m x v - \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} t\right) \right] \hat {T} (v t) \\ = \hat {T} (v t) \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m x v + \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} t\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\hat { T }$ 是空间平移算符(方程 (6.3)). 你将会用到贝克一坎贝尔一豪斯多夫公式(习3.29).

(c) 证明:如果 $\varPsi$ 是哈密顿量

$$
\hat {H} = \frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} + V (x)
$$

的含时薛定谆方程的解.那么，伽利略变换下的波函数 ${ \varPsi } ^ { \prime } = \hat { \Gamma } \left( v , t \right) \varPsi$ 也是运动势$V \left( x \right)$ 的含时薛定博方程的解:

$$
\hat {H} = \frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} + V (x - v t).
$$

注释仅当 $\left[ { \hat { A } } , \mathrm { d } { \hat { A } } / \mathrm { d } t \right] = 0$ 时， $\left( \mathrm { d } / \mathrm { d } t \right) \mathrm { e } ^ { \hat { A } } = \mathrm { e } ^ { \hat { A } } \left( \mathrm { d } \hat { A } / \mathrm { d } t \right)$

(d) 证明:习题 2.50(a) 的结论就是本题结论的一个例子.

解答 (时对于一个无限小的速度，伽利略变换的么正算符为

$$
\hat {\Gamma} (v, t) = \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} v (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \right] = 1 - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}).
$$

因此，伽利略变换下坐标算符为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {x} ^ {\prime} = \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\dagger} \hat {x} \hat {\Gamma} = \left[ 1 + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \right] \hat {x} \left[ 1 - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \right] \\ = \hat {x} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \hat {x} - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \hat {x} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \\ = \hat {x} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta t \hat {p} \hat {x} - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta m \hat {x} ^ {2} - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta t \hat {p} \hat {x} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta m \hat {x} ^ {2} \\ = \hat {x} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta t [ \hat {p}, \hat {x} ] \\ = \hat {x} + \delta t. \\ \end{array}
$$

同理，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {p} ^ {\prime} = \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\dagger} \hat {p} \hat {\Gamma} = \left[ 1 + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \right] \hat {p} \left[ 1 - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \right] \\ = \hat {p} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \hat {p} - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \hat {p} \delta (t \hat {p} - m \hat {x}) \\ = \hat {p} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta t \hat {p} ^ {2} - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta m \hat {x} \hat {p} - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta t \hat {p} ^ {2} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta m \hat {p} \hat {x} \\ = \hat {p} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \delta m [ \hat {p}, \hat {x} ] \\ = \hat {p} + \delta m. \\ \end{array}
$$

$\hat { x } ^ { \prime } = \hat { x } + \delta t$ 变换后的坐标系是变换前的坐标系以速度 $\delta$ 运动时间 $t$ 后的情况 $\hat { p } ^ { \prime } = \hat { p } + \delta m$ 动量增加 $\delta m$

(b) 构建两个算符

$$
\hat {A} = - \frac {\mathrm {i} v t}{\hbar} \hat {p}, \quad \hat {B} = \frac {\mathrm {i} m \hat {x} v}{\hbar}.
$$

它们的对易关系为

$$
\hat {C} = \left[ \hat {A}, \hat {B} \right] = \frac {m v ^ {2} t}{\hbar^ {2}} \left[ \hat {p}, \hat {x} \right] = - \frac {\mathrm {i} m v ^ {2} t}{\hbar}.
$$

根据贝克-坎贝尔-豪斯多夫公式

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {\Gamma} (v, t) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {A} + \hat {B}} = \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \hat {C} / 2} \\ = \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} v t \hat {p} \right] \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t \right] \\ = \hat {T} (v t) \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v + \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} t\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

将算符 $\hat { A }$ 与算符 $\hat { B }$ 互换，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {\Gamma} (v, t) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {A} + \hat {B}} = \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \hat {C} / 2} \\ = \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} v t \hat {p} \right] \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t \right] \\ = \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t \right] \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} v t \hat {p} \right] \\ = \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v - \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] \hat {T} (v t). \\ \end{array}
$$

(c) 薛定愕方程为

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \Psi = H \Psi .
$$

伽利略变换后的波函数为雪 $\psi ^ { \prime } = \hat { \Gamma } \psi$ 所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \left(\hat {\Gamma} \Psi\right) = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \hat {\Gamma}}{\partial t} \Psi + \hat {\Gamma} \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t} \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \hat {\Gamma}}{\partial t} \Psi + \hat {\Gamma} H \Psi \\ = \left(\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \hat {\Gamma}}{\partial t} \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\dagger} + \hat {\Gamma} H \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\dagger}\right) \hat {\Gamma} \Psi . \\ \end{array}
$$

因此， $\varPsi ^ { \prime }$ 满足的晗密顿量为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {H} ^ {\prime} = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \hat {\Gamma}}{\partial t} \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\dagger} + \hat {\Gamma} H \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\dagger} \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \hat {\Gamma}}{\partial t} \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\dagger} + \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v - \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] \hat {T} (v t) H \hat {T} ^ {\dagger} (v t) \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v - \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \hat {\Gamma}}{\partial t} \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\dagger} + \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \left[ \frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} + V (x - v t) \right] \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

由

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d} \hat {\Gamma} ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm {d} t} = \left[ \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \hat {T} (v t) \right] \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v + \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] + \hat {T} (v t) \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v + \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} v p \hat {T} (v t) \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v + \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] + \hat {T} (v t) \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(\frac {m v ^ {2}}{2}\right) \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v + \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(v p - \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2}\right) \hat {\Gamma}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以， $\mathrm { i } \hbar \frac { \partial \hat { \Gamma } ^ { \prime } } { \partial t } \hat { \Gamma } ^ { \dagger } = v p - \frac { m v ^ { 2 } } { 2 }$ 'f F= 叩 mu2

$$
\begin{array}{l} \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \left[ \frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} + V (\hat {x} - v t) \right] \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \hat {p} ^ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] + V (\hat {x} - v t) \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \hat {p} \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] \hat {p} \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} m \hat {x} v \right] + V (\hat {x} - v t). \\ \end{array}
$$

借鉴

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} m \hat {x} v / \hbar}, \hat {p} \right] = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{n !} \left(\frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar}\right) ^ {n} [ \hat {x} ^ {n}, \hat {p} ] \\ = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{n !} \left(\frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar}\right) ^ {n} \mathrm {i} \hbar n \hat {x} ^ {n - 1} \\ = - m v \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{(n - 1) !} \left(\frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar} \hat {x}\right) ^ {n - 1} \\ = - m v \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} m \hat {x} v / \hbar}, \\ \end{array}
$$

可以得出

$$
\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} m \hat {x} v / \hbar} \hat {p} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} m \hat {x} v / \hbar} = \hat {p} - m v.
$$

结合以上过程，变换后的哈密顿量为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {H} ^ {\prime} = \frac {1}{2 m} (\hat {p} - m v) ^ {2} + V (\hat {x} - v t) + v \hat {p} - \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \hat {p} ^ {2} + V (\hat {x} - v t). \\ \end{array}
$$

定态薛定诗方程的解为

$$
\varPsi (x, t) = \psi_ {0} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E t / \hbar},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi^ {\prime} (x, t) = \hat {\Gamma} \psi_ {0} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E t / \hbar} \\ = \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v - \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] \hat {T} (v t) \frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar} \exp \left[ - m \alpha | \hat {x} | ^ {2} / \hbar^ {2} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E t / \hbar} \\ = \exp \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left(m \hat {x} v - \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2} t\right) \right] \frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar} \exp \left[ - m \alpha | \hat {x} - v t | ^ {2} / \hbar^ {2} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E t / \hbar} \\ = \frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar} \exp \left[ - m \alpha | \hat {x} - v t | ^ {2} / \hbar^ {2} \right] \exp \left\{- \mathrm {i} \left[ \left(E + \frac {m v ^ {2}}{2}\right) t - m \hat {x} v \right] / \hbar \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

这与习题 2.50(a) 一致.

习题 在位置 $r _ { 0 }$ 处你以速度。 $\pmb { v } _ { 0 }$ 向空中抛出一个球，经过 $t$ 时间后到达 $\mathbf { \Delta } _ { \mathbf { r } _ { 1 } }$ 处，且速度为。 ${ \pmb v } _ { 1 }$ 假如在球到达 $\mathbf { \Delta } _ { \mathbf { r } _ { 1 } }$ 处时，瞬时反转球的速度，忽略空气阻力，球将以速度 $- { \pmb v } _ { 1 }$ 沿着原路径从 $\mathbf { \Delta } _ { \mathbf { r } _ { 1 } }$ 开始，经过一个时间 $t$ 后重返位置 $\mathbf { \boldsymbol { r } } _ { 0 }$ 处.此为时间反演不变性的一个例子一一使粒子在其轨迹上的任一点上的运动方向反向，它将在所有位置以大小相等但方向相反的速度沿原来的路径返回.

为什么这被称作时间反演?毕竟，反转的是速度，而不是时问如果给你看球从 $\mathbf { \Delta } _ { \mathbf { r } _ { 1 } }$ 运行到 $\mathbf { \boldsymbol { r } } _ { 0 }$ 的电影，你就无法判断你是在看反转后向前打球的电影还是反转前向后打球的电影.在时间反演不变的系统中，倒放电影代表着另外一种可能的运动

一个常见的没有时间反演对称性的例子是带电粒子在外磁场中运动.①在这种情况下，当反转粒子的速度时，洛伦兹力会改变符号，粒子不再沿着以前的路径返回;如图

时间反演算符 $\hat { \Theta }$ 是一个让粒子动量反转$\left( p \to - p \right)$ 且位置保持不变的算符.其实更好的名称应该是"运动方向反转"算符l2l对于无

![](images/7c209b8432b5ac07f87326812b6d9bfb1011c11e71794b2eb7d49a27def1a217.jpg)  
.4 以扔球作为一个时间反演不变性的例子(忽略安气阻力).如果在轨迹的任一点反转粒子的速度，它将重复这条轨迹

自旋粒子，时间反演算符 $\hat { \Theta }$ 是简单地把位置空间波函数取复共辄@

$$
\hat {\Theta} \Psi (x, t) = \Psi^ {*} (x, t).
$$

![](images/6d18348cde9154f9f682a38d938fc6fd97ebd8fe78711302f214937e7e47c10e.jpg)

![](images/6a175dc5901d2956f135b9e79b82fe53ad408e2bb54191752f0d928222d5e391.jpg)  
6.5 外磁场打破时间反演对称性.圄为带电量为 $+ q$ 的粒子在垂直纸面向里的均匀磁场中运动.如果粒子的速度从 ${ \pmb v } _ { 1 }$ 到 $- \pmb { v } _ { 1 }$ 发生反转，粒子不再重复以前的路径，而是进入一个新的圆形轨道

(a) 证明:在时间反演操作下，算符 $\hat { x }$ 和 $\hat { p }$ 的变换是

$$
\hat {x} ^ {\prime} = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} \hat {x} \hat {\Theta} = \hat {x},
$$

$$
\hat {p} ^ {\prime} = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} \hat {p} \hat {\Theta} = - \hat {p}.
$$

$$
\langle \Theta f | \Theta g \rangle = \langle f | g \rangle^ {*}
$$

$$
\hat {\Theta} (a | \alpha \rangle + b | \beta \rangle) = a ^ {*} \hat {\Theta} | \alpha \rangle + b ^ {*} \hat {\Theta} | \beta \rangle
$$

而么正算符满足同样的两个方程，但不是复共辄.这里我不去定义反么正算符的共辄算符;相反，我使用 $\Theta ^ { - 1 }$ 表示反么正算符，其中我们可以互换地使用 $\hat { U } ^ { \dag }$ 或 $\hat { U } ^ { - 1 }$ 表示么正算符

提示 通过计算算符 $\hat { x } ^ { \prime }$ '和 $\hat { p } ^ { \prime }$ 作用在任意试探函数 $f \left( x \right)$ 上来实现这一点，

(b) 可以从上面的讨论中写出时间反演不变性的数学形式.选取一个系统，让其演化一段时间 $t$ 然后再让其动量反转井再次演化一段时间 $t$ 如果系统具有时间反演不变性，尽管动量反转(图 .4)，系统将返回到出发的地方.用算符来描述是

$$
\hat {U} (t) \hat {\Theta} \hat {U} (t) = \hat {\Theta}.
$$

如果这对任意时间间隔都成立，那么它必须对无穷小的时间间隔 $\delta$ 也成立.证明:时间反演不变性要求

$$
\left[ \hat {\Theta}, \hat {H} \right] = 0. \tag {6.83}
$$

(c) 证明:对于时间反转不变哈密顿量，如果 $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ 是能量为 $E _ { n }$ 的定态，则$\psi _ { n } ^ { * } \left( x \right)$ 也是一个具有相同能量 $E _ { n }$ 的定态.如果能量是非简并的，这意味着定态可以选择为实的.  
对动量本征函数 $f _ { p } \left( x \right)$ 作时间反演后会得到什么(方程 波函数为氢原子波函数 $\psi _ { n \ell m } \left( r , \theta , \phi \right)$ >)会怎样?讨论每个态与没有变换态的关系，如 (c) 所确定的，验证变换态和未变换态具有相同能量.

解答 (时常用的做法是引入一个试探函数 $f \left( x \right)$

$$
\hat {x} ^ {\prime} f (x) = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} \hat {x} \hat {\Theta} f (x) = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} \hat {x} f ^ {*} (x) = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} x f ^ {*} (x) = x \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} f ^ {*} (x) = x [ f ^ {*} (x) ] ^ {*} = x f (x) = \hat {x} f (x).
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {p} ^ {\prime} f (x) = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} \hat {p} \hat {\Theta} f (x) = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} \hat {p} f ^ {*} (x) = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) f ^ {*} (x) \\ = \hat {\Theta} ^ {- 1} \left[ \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} f (x) \right] ^ {*} = \left[ \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} f (x) \right] = - \hat {p} f (x). \\ \end{array}
$$

即 $\hat { x } ^ { \prime } = \hat { \Theta } ^ { - 1 } \hat { x } \hat { \Theta } = \hat { x }$ $\hat { p } ^ { \prime } = \hat { \Theta } ^ { - 1 } \hat { p } \hat { \Theta } = - \hat { p }$ .

例由 $\hat { U } \left( \delta \right) = \exp \left[ - \mathrm { i } \frac { \delta } { \hbar } \hat { H } \right] = 1 - \mathrm { i } \frac { \delta } { \hbar } \hat { H }$ ； 得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {U} (\delta) \hat {\Theta} \hat {U} (\delta) = \left(1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \hat {H}\right) \hat {\Theta} \left(1 - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \hat {H}\right) \\ = \hat {\Theta} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \hat {H} \hat {\Theta} + \hat {\Theta} \left(- \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \hat {H}\right) \\ = \hat {\Theta} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \hat {H} \hat {\Theta} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \hat {\Theta} \hat {H} \\ = \hat {\Theta} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\delta}{\hbar} \left[ \hat {H}, \hat {\Theta} \right] \\ \mathbf {\Sigma} = \hat {\Theta}, \\ \end{array}
$$

即 $\left[ { \hat { H } } , { \hat { \Theta } } \right] = 0$

(付果 $\hat { H } \psi _ { n } = E _ { n } \psi _ { n } , \Big [ \hat { H } , \hat { \Theta } \Big ] = 0$ $\left[ { \hat { H } } , { \hat { \Theta } } \right] = 0$ ，那么

$$
\hat {H} \psi_ {n} ^ {*} = \hat {H} \hat {\Theta} \psi_ {n} = \hat {\Theta} \hat {H} \psi_ {n} = \hat {\Theta} E _ {n} \psi_ {n} = (E _ {n} \psi_ {n}) ^ {*} = E _ {n} \psi_ {n} ^ {*}.
$$

能量非简井的情况下， $\psi _ { n } ^ { * } = c \psi _ { n }$ ，其中 $c$ 选为常实数.所以 $\psi _ { n }$ 可以选为实的

(d) 时间反演算符作用到动量本征态上

$$
\hat {\Theta} f _ {p} (x) = \hat {\Theta} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} = f _ {- p} (x).
$$

对于氢原子披函数(参考方程 (4.32) $Y _ { \ell } ^ { m }$ 的表达式)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {\Theta} \psi_ {n \ell m} (r) = \hat {\Theta} R _ {n \ell} (r) Y _ {\ell} ^ {m} (\theta , \varphi) \\ = R _ {n \ell} (r) \hat {\Theta} Y _ {\ell} ^ {m} (\theta , \varphi) \\ = R _ {n \ell} (r) (- 1) ^ {m} Y _ {\ell} ^ {- m} (\theta , \varphi) \\ = (- 1) ^ {m} \psi_ {n, \ell , - m} (r). \\ \end{array}
$$

这两个态为筒并态，描述粒子的运动方向相反.

习题 6.37 自旋作为粒子的角动量，它在时间反演作用下一定反转(习题 6.36).间反演对旋量的作用 (4.4.1 节)实际上是

$$
\hat {\Theta} \left( \begin{array}{c} a \\ b \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c} - b ^ {*} \\ a ^ {*} \end{array} \right).
$$

(a) 证明:对于自旋为 $1 / 2$ 的粒子， $\hat { \Theta } ^ { 2 } = - 1$   
(b) 考虑能量为 $E _ { n }$ 的具有时间反演不变的哈密顿量(方程 (6.83)) 的本征态$\left| { \psi _ { n } } \right.$ ).我们知道， $| \psi _ { n } ^ { \prime } \rangle = \hat { \Theta } | \psi _ { n } \rangle$ 也是 $\hat { H }$ 的一个能量为 $E _ { n }$ 的本征态.有两种可能性:要么 $\left| { \psi _ { n } } \right.$ 和 $\left| \psi _ { n } ^ { \prime } \right.$ 是相同的态(这意味着 $| \psi _ { n } ^ { \prime } \rangle = c | \psi _ { n } \rangle$ ，其中 $c$ 为某个复常数)，要么它们是不同的态.证明:对于自旋为 $1 / 2$ 的粒子，第一种情况会导致矛盾，也就是说能级必须(至少)是二重简井的.

注释所证明的是克拉默简井性的一个特例:对于自旋为 $1 / 2$ 奇数倍的粒子(或任何半整数自旋)， (时间反演不变的哈密顿量)每个能级至少是二重简井的.如同你所证明的那样，这是因为半整数自旋态和它的时间反演态一定是不同的.①

Rn a mE $\left( \begin{array} { l } { a } \\ { b } \end{array} \right)$

$$
\hat {\Theta} ^ {2} \left( \begin{array}{c} a \\ b \end{array} \right) = \hat {\Theta} \left( \begin{array}{c} - b ^ {*} \\ a ^ {*} \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c} - (a ^ {*}) ^ {*} \\ (- b ^ {*}) ^ {*} \end{array} \right) = - \left( \begin{array}{c} a \\ b \end{array} \right).
$$

(b) 假设 $| \psi _ { \ n } ^ { \prime } \rangle = c | \psi _ { n } \rangle$ ，即 $\left| { \psi _ { n } ^ { \prime } } \right.$ $\left| { \psi _ { n } } \right.$ ;是相同的态，则

$$
\hat {\Theta} ^ {2} | \psi_ {n} \rangle = \hat {\Theta} | \psi_ {n} ^ {\prime} \rangle = \hat {\Theta} c | \psi_ {n} \rangle = c ^ {*} \hat {\Theta} | \psi_ {n} \rangle = c ^ {*} | \psi_ {n} ^ {\prime} \rangle = c ^ {*} c | \psi_ {n} \rangle = | c | ^ {2} | \psi_ {n} \rangle .
$$

由(帆，对于自旋为 $\frac { 1 } { 2 }$ 的粒子， $\hat { \Theta } ^ { 2 } = - 1$ ，这与 $| c | ^ { 2 }$ 不能为负值相矛盾因此，能级至少是二重筒并的
