\begin{array}{l} E _ {0} ^ {2} = \left\langle \psi_ {0} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {0} ^ {1} \right\rangle \\ = \int \left(\frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a}}{\sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}}\right) \left(- \frac {e p \cos \theta}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2}}\right) \left(\frac {m e p}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \hbar^ {2} \sqrt {\pi a ^ {3}}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- r / a}\right) \cos \theta r ^ {2} \sin \theta \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {d} \theta \mathrm {d} \phi \\ = - \frac {2 \pi m e ^ {2} p ^ {2}}{\left(4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \hbar\right) ^ {2} \pi a ^ {3}} \underbrace {\int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r / a} \mathrm {d} r} _ {a / 2} \underbrace {\int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \sin \theta \cos^ {2} \theta \mathrm {d} \theta} _ {2 / 3} \\ = - \frac {2}{3} m \left(\frac {e p}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \hbar a}\right) ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

代入玻尔基态能量 $E _ { 1 } = - \frac { m e ^ { 4 } } { 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } \left( 4 \pi \varepsilon _ { 0 } \right) ^ { 2 } }$ mé 一 能量的二阶修正可以表示为

$$
E _ {0} ^ {2} = \frac {4}{3} \left(\frac {p}{e a}\right) ^ {2} E _ {1}.
$$

习题 7.52 质量为 $m$ 、带电荷 $q$ 的无自旋带电粒子在二维谐振子势的作用下，在xy 平面内运动，势能为

$$
V (x, y) = \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}).
$$

构建基态波函数 $\psi _ { 0 } \left( x , y \right)$ ，并写出其能量.同样地，求(简并)第一激发态的波函数和能量.  
(b) 假设在 $z$ 方向加一个微弱的外磁场 $B _ { 0 }$ ，因此(对于一阶的 $B _ { 0 }$ ) 哈密顿量获得了一个额外的项

$$
H ^ {\prime} = - \boldsymbol {\mu} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} = - \frac {q}{2 m} (\boldsymbol {L} \cdot \boldsymbol {B}) = - \frac {q B _ {0}}{2 m} (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}).
$$

将此项作为微扰，求基态和第一激发态能量的一阶修正.

解答 (a) 二维无精合项的谐振子的波函数可用分离变量法写成两个一维谐振子被函数之积

$$
\psi_ {m n} (x, y) = \psi_ {m} (x) \psi_ {n} (y),
$$

能量也为两个一维谐振子能量之和

$$
E _ {m, n} = E _ {m} + E _ {n} = \left(m + \frac {1}{2}\right) \hbar \omega + \left(n + \frac {1}{2}\right) \hbar \omega = (m + n + 1) \hbar \omega .
$$

基态情况下， $\begin{array} { r } {  { m } =  { n } = \phantom { - } 0 , E _ { 0 , 0 } = \hbar \omega , \psi _ { 0 , 0 } ( x , y ) = \psi _ { 0 } ( x ) \psi _ { 0 } ( y ) } \end{array}$ ，由方程 (2.60) $\psi _ { 0 } \left( x \right) =$ $\left( { \frac { m \omega } { \pi \hbar } } \right) ^ { 1 / 4 } \mathrm { e } ^ { - { \frac { m \omega } { 2 \hbar } } x ^ { 2 } }$ ，所以

$$
\psi_ {0, 0} (x, y) = \psi_ {0} (x) \psi_ {0} (y) = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})}.
$$

第一激发态有两种情况

$m = 1 , n = 0$ 时，

$$
E _ {1, 0} = (1 + 0 + 1) \hbar \omega = 2 \hbar \omega .
$$

(2.6臼 $\psi _ { 1 } \left( x \right) = \left( { \frac { m \omega } { \pi \hbar } } \right) ^ { 1 / 4 } { \sqrt { \frac { 2 m \omega } { \hbar } } } x \mathrm { e } ^ { - { \frac { m \omega } { 2 \hbar } } x ^ { 2 } }$ m2 ，所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi_ {1, 0} (x, y) = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x ^ {2}} \cdot \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} y ^ {2}} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$m = 0 , n = 1$ 时，

$$
E _ {0, 1} = (0 + 1 + 1) \hbar \omega = 2 \hbar \omega .
$$

同理，被函数为

$$
\psi_ {0, 1} (x, y) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) y \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})}.
$$

(b) 基态能量的一阶修正为

$$
\begin{array}{l} E _ {0} ^ {1} = \left\langle \psi_ {0, 0} \right| H ^ {\prime} | \psi_ {0, 0} \rangle \\ = \iint \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})} \left[ - \frac {q B _ {0}}{2 m} (x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}) \right] \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})} \mathrm {d} x \mathrm {d} y \\ = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) \cdot \left(- \frac {q B _ {0}}{2 m}\right) \cdot \iint \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \left(x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \mathrm {d} x \mathrm {d} y. \\ \end{array}
$$

将 $p _ { x }$ 和 $p _ { y }$ 当作算符向后作用

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} = \left[ x \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial y}\right) - y \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial x}\right) \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \left(x \frac {\partial}{\partial y} - y \frac {\partial}{\partial x}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m _ {\omega}}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \left[ x \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} y\right) - y \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x\right) \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \\ = 0, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以 $E _ { 0 } ^ { 1 } = 0$

对于第一激发态，它是二重筒并的，因此需要用到简并微扰理论构造 $w$ 矩阵，先求解 $w$ 矩阵元，令 $\psi _ { 1 } ^ { a } = \psi _ { 1 , 0 } , \psi _ { 1 } ^ { b } = \psi _ { 0 , 1 }$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} W _ {a a} = \left\langle \psi_ {1} ^ {a} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {1} ^ {a} \right\rangle \\ = \iint \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \\ \times \left[ - \frac {q B _ {0}}{2 m} \left(x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}\right) \right] \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \mathrm {d} x \mathrm {d} y \\ = \frac {2}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \cdot \left(- \frac {q B _ {0}}{2 m}\right) \cdot \iint x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \left(x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}\right) x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \mathrm {d} x \mathrm {d} y. \\ \end{array}
$$

将 $p _ { x }$ 和 $p _ { y }$ 当作算符向后作用，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(x p _ {y} - y p _ {x}\right) \left[ x e ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \right] \\ = \left[ x \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial y}\right) - y \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial x}\right) \right] \left[ x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \right] \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \left(x \frac {\partial}{\partial y} - y \frac {\partial}{\partial x}\right) \left[ x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \right] \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \left\{x ^ {2} \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} y\right) - \left[ y + x y \cdot \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x\right) \right] \right\} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x ^ {2} y - y + \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x ^ {2} y\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar y \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
W _ {a a} = \frac {2}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \cdot \left(- \frac {q B _ {0}}{2 m}\right) \cdot \iint x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} (- \mathrm {i} \hbar y) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \mathrm {d} x \mathrm {d} y
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {\mathrm {i} m \omega^ {2} q B _ {0}}{\pi \hbar} \iint x y \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \left(x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}\right)} \mathrm {d} x \mathrm {d} y \\ = \frac {\mathrm {i} m \omega^ {2} q B _ {0}}{\pi \hbar} \int x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \int y \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} y ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} y \\ = 0 \quad (\text {用 到 了 奇 函 数 在 对 称 区 间 上 积 分 的 性 质}, \text {同 理}, W _ {b b} = 0). \\ \end{array}
$$

而

$$
\begin{array}{l} W _ {b a} = \left\langle \psi_ {1} ^ {b} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {1} ^ {a} \right\rangle \\ = \frac {2}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \cdot \left(- \frac {q B _ {0}}{2 m}\right) \cdot (- \mathrm {i} \hbar) \iint x ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})} \mathrm {d} x \mathrm {d} y \\ = \mathrm {i} \left(\frac {m \omega^ {2} q B _ {0}}{\pi \hbar}\right) \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \sqrt {\frac {\pi \hbar}{m \omega}}\right) \left(\sqrt {\frac {\pi \hbar}{m \omega}}\right) \\ = \mathrm {i} \left(\frac {q B _ {0} \hbar}{2 m}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

则

$$
W _ {a b} = W _ {b a} ^ {*} = - \mathrm {i} \left(\frac {q B _ {0} \hbar}{2 m}\right).
$$

所以

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {W}} = \left| \begin{array}{c c} W _ {a a} & W _ {a b} \\ W _ {b a} & W _ {b b} \end{array} \right| = \left| \begin{array}{c c} 0 & - \mathrm {i} \frac {q B _ {0} \hbar}{2 m} \\ \mathrm {i} \frac {q B _ {0} \hbar}{2 m} & 0 \end{array} \right| = \mathrm {i} \frac {q B _ {0} \hbar}{2 m} \left| \begin{array}{c c} 0 & - 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{array} \right|.
$$

解久期方程 $\left| \begin{array} { c c } { { - \lambda } } & { { - 1 } } \\ { { 1 } } & { { - \lambda } } \end{array} \right| = 0 , \lambda ^ { 2 } + 1 = 0 , \lambda = \pm \mathrm { i } .$

$\lambda = - \mathrm { i }$ 时， $E _ { + } ^ { 1 } = \frac { q B _ { 0 } \hbar } { 2 m }$

当 $\lambda = \mathrm { i }$ 时. $E _ { - } ^ { 1 } = - \frac { q B _ { 0 } \hbar } { 2 m }$

假设在无限深方势阱(方程 (2.22)) 中引入一个 事** 叫主 习题 7.53 $\delta$ 函数微扰，

$$
H ^ {\prime} (x) = \lambda \delta (x - x _ {0}),
$$

其中 $\lambda$ 是一正常数，且 $0 < x _ { 0 } < a$ (为简化问题，令 $x _ { 0 } = p a$ ，其中 $0 < p < 1$ .

假定 $\lambda$ 很小，求第 $n$ 个能量允许值(方程 的一阶修正. (在上下文"小"的含义是什么  
(b) 求能量允许值的二阶修正. (将结果表示为求和形式.)  
(c) 精确求解薛定谆方程，分别考虑。 $0 \leqslant x < x _ { 0 }$ 和 $x _ { 0 } < x < a$ 区间，在 $x _ { 0 }$ 处施加边界条件.推导出能量的超越方程:

$$
u _ {n} \sin (u _ {n}) + \Lambda \sin (p u _ {n}) \sin [ (1 - p) u _ {n} ] = 0, \quad (E > 0) \tag {7.116}
$$

其中 $\varLambda \equiv 2 m a \lambda / \hbar ^ { 2 }$ $u _ { n } \equiv k _ { n } a$ 和 $k _ { n } \equiv \sqrt { 2 m E _ { n } } / \hbar$ 在适当限制条件下，验证方程(7.116) 是否在合适的限制条件下重现了 (a) 部分中的结果.

(d) 如果 $\lambda$ 为负，所有这些都成立，但在这种情况下，可能会出现一个负能量的额外解.推导出一个负能态的超越方程:

$$
\nu \sinh (\nu) + \Lambda \sinh (p \nu) \sinh [ (1 - p) \nu ] = 0 \quad (E <   0),
$$

其中 $\nu \equiv k a$ 和 $k \equiv \sqrt { - 2 m E } / \hbar$ 对于 $p = 1 / 2$ 的对称情况，证明:在适当的区域你可以恢复 $\delta$ 函数势阱(方程 (2.132)) 的能量

(e) 事实上，如果 $| A | > 1 / [ p ( 1 - p ) ]$ 存在一个负能量解.首先，对 $p = 1 / 2$ 时的情况(图像地)证明. (低于这个临界值，负能量解不存在.)下一步，对于 $\varLambda =$ $- 4 . 1 , - 5$ 和 $- 1 0$ ，用计算机画出 $p$ 的函数的解 $\nu$ 验证该解仅存在于 $p$ 的预测范围内  
(。对于 $p = 1 / 2$ ，画出 $\varLambda = 0 , - 2 , - 3 , - 3 . 5 , - 4 . 5 , - 5$ 和 $- 1 0$ 情况下的基态波函数，演示随着 $\delta$ 势阱函数的深度变化，波函数是怎样由正弦形状(教材图 2.2)化为指数形状的(教材图 2.13).

解答(时一维无限时酬的波函数 $\psi _ { n } ^ { 0 } = { \sqrt { \frac { 2 } { a } } } { \frac { \sin n \pi x } { a } }$ 能量为 $E _ { n } ^ { 0 } = { \frac { n ^ { 2 } \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } } }$ 第 $n$ 个本征能量 $E _ { n } ^ { 0 } = { \frac { n ^ { 2 } \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } } }$ 的一阶微扰为

$$
\begin{array}{l} E _ {n} ^ {1} = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle \\ = \int \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right) \cdot \lambda \delta (x - x _ {0}) \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right) d x \\ = \frac {2}{a} \lambda \int \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right) \delta \left(x - x _ {0}\right) \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right) d x \\ = \frac {2 \lambda}{a} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

其中用到 $\delta$ 函数的积分性质.

$\lambda$ 很民刷忡味着翻矩阵阳元 $| V _ { m n } | = \left( { \frac { 2 \lambda } { a } } \right) \left| \sin \left( { \frac { m \pi x _ { 0 } } { a } } \right) \cdot \sin \left( { \frac { n \pi x _ { 0 } } { a } } \right) \right|$ 问与能级刷问隔翩相比帧较制小、

$$
\left(\frac {2 \lambda}{a}\right) / \left(\frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}\right) \ll 1,
$$

所以 $\lambda \ll \frac { a } { 2 }$ .' $\frac { \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } } = \frac { \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 4 m a }$

(b) 微扰矩阵元为

$$
\begin{array}{l} V _ {m n} = \left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \mid H ^ {\prime} \mid \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle \\ = \frac {2 \lambda}{a} \sin \left(\frac {m \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right) \cdot \sin \left(\frac {n \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

由工阶微扰理论，得

$$
E _ {n} ^ {2} = \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| V _ {m n} \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left(\frac {2 \lambda}{a}\right) ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {m \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right) \cdot \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right)}{\frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} \left(n ^ {2} - m ^ {2}\right)} \\ = \left(\frac {2 \lambda}{a}\right) ^ {2} \cdot \frac {2 m a ^ {2}}{\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\sin^ {2} \left(\frac {m \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right) \cdot \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right)}{n ^ {2} - m ^ {2}} \\ = \frac {8 m \lambda^ {2}}{\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right) \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\sin^ {2} \left(\frac {m \pi x _ {0}}{a}\right)}{n ^ {2} - m ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(c) 分段求解定态薛定诲方程

在 $0 \leqslant x < x _ { 0 }$ 和 $x _ { 0 } < x < a$ 时, $V \left( x \right) = 0$ . 方程为

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} \psi (x) = E \psi (x),
$$

通解为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = A \sin \left(k x\right) + B \cos \left(k x\right), \text {其 中} k = \sqrt {\frac {2 m E}{\hbar^ {2}}},
$$

分段解可写为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} A \sin \left(k x\right) + B \cos \left(k x\right), & 0 \leqslant x <   x _ {0}, \\ C \sin \left(k x\right) + D \cos \left(k x\right), & x _ {0} \leqslant x <   a. \end{array} \right.
$$

由边界条件 $x = 0 , \psi \left( x \right) = 0$ 得 $B = 0$ ，由 $x = a , \psi \left( a \right) = 0$ 得 $D = - C \tan \left( k a \right)$

因此，在 $x _ { 0 } < x < a$ 时，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi (x) = - C \tan (k a) \cos (k x) + C \sin (k x) \\ = \frac {- C}{\cos (k a)} [ \sin (k a) \cos (k x) - \cos (k a) \sin (k x) ] \\ = \frac {- C}{\cos (k a)} \sin (k a - k x) \\ = C ^ {\prime} \sin [ k (a - x) ]. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} A \sin \left(k x\right), & 0 \leqslant x <   x _ {0} \\ C ^ {\prime} \sin \left[ k \left(a - x\right) \right], & x _ {0} \leqslant x <   a \end{array} \right.
$$

其中 $C ^ { \prime } = - \frac { C } { \cos \left( k a \right) }$

由波函数连续性条件，得

$$
A \sin (k x _ {0}) = C ^ {\prime} \sin [ k (a - x _ {0}) ]. \tag {①}
$$

一阶微分连续，由方程 (2.128) ，得

$$
\Delta \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) = \frac {2 m \lambda}{\hbar^ {2}} \psi (x _ {0}),
$$

IlP

$$
- C ^ {\prime} k \cos [ k (a - x _ {0}) ] - A k \cos (k x _ {0}) = \frac {2 m \lambda}{\hbar^ {2}} A \sin (k x _ {0}). \tag {②}
$$

联立 $\textcircled{1}$ $\textcircled{2}$ ，得

$$
k \cot (k x _ {0}) + \frac {2 m \lambda}{\hbar^ {2}} = - k \cot [ k (a - x _ {0}) ],
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} u \cot (p u) + \Lambda = - u \cot [ u (1 - p) ], \\ u \cos (p u) \sin [ u (1 - p) ] + \Lambda \sin (p u) \sin [ u (1 - p) ] = - u \cos [ u (q - p) ] \sin (p u). \\ \end{array}
$$

化简为

$$
u \sin (u) + \Lambda \sin (p u) \sin [ u (1 - p) ] = 0, \quad u = \sqrt {\frac {2 m a ^ {2} E}{\hbar^ {2}}}.
$$

求能量修正

$$
\begin{array}{l} u = \sqrt {\frac {2 m a ^ {2} E}{\hbar^ {2}}} = \sqrt {\frac {2 m a ^ {2} \left(E _ {n} ^ {0} + E _ {n} ^ {1} + \cdots\right)}{\hbar^ {2}}} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {2 m a ^ {2} E _ {n} ^ {0}}{\hbar^ {2}}} + \sqrt {\frac {2 m a ^ {2} E _ {n} ^ {1}}{\hbar^ {2}}} + \dots \\ = n \pi + \frac {m a ^ {2}}{n \pi \hbar^ {2}} E _ {n} ^ {1} + \dots \\ = u _ {0} + \delta u. \\ \end{array}
$$

将 $u = u _ { 0 } + \delta u$ 代入 $u \sin \left( u \right) + A \sin \left( p u \right) \sin \left[ u \left( 1 - p \right) \right] = 0$ ，得

$$
(u _ {0} + \delta u) \sin (u _ {0} + \delta u) + \Lambda \sin (p u _ {0}) \sin [ u _ {0} (1 - p) ] = 0.
$$

忽略 $\delta ^ { 2 } u$ 项，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} u _ {0} \sin (u _ {0}) + [ u _ {0} \cos (u _ {0}) + \sin (u _ {0}) ] \delta u + \Lambda \sin (p u _ {0}) \approx 0, \\ \delta u = \frac {m a ^ {2}}{n \pi \hbar^ {2}} E _ {n} ^ {1} = - (- 1) ^ {n} \frac {\Lambda}{u _ {0}} \sin (p u _ {0}) \sin [ u _ {0} (1 - p) ]. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} E _ {n} ^ {1} = - (- 1) ^ {n} \frac {n \pi \hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {2}} \frac {2 m a \lambda}{n \pi \hbar^ {2}} \sin (p u _ {0}) [ \sin u _ {0} \cos (p u _ {0}) - \sin (p u _ {0}) \cos u _ {0} ] \\ = \frac {2 \lambda}{a} [ \sin (p u _ {0}) ] ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由于 $p u _ { 0 } = { \frac { n { \mathfrak { \pi } } x _ { 0 } } { a } }$ 所以这与 中的结论一致

(d) 考虑到能量为负值的情况

$$
u = \sqrt {\frac {2 m E a ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}}} = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {2 m | E | a ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}}} = \mathrm {i} \nu .
$$

将其代入 $u \sin { ( u ) } + A \sin { ( p u ) } \sin { [ u ( 1 - p ) ] } = 0$ 中，且由 $\sin { ( \mathrm { i } z ) } = \mathrm { i } \sinh { ( z ) }$ ，得

$$
u \sinh (\nu) + \Lambda \sinh (p \nu) \sinh [ (1 - p) \nu ] = 0.
$$

对于 $p = 1 / 2$ 的情况

$$
\nu \sinh \nu + \varLambda \sinh^ {2} \left(\frac {\nu}{2}\right) = 0,
$$

$$
\nu = - \frac {1}{2} \Lambda \tanh \left(\frac {\nu}{2}\right).
$$

如果 $a$ 足够大，那么边界就不再重要，所以 $\operatorname { t a n h } \left( { \frac { \nu } { 2 } } \right) = \operatorname { t a n h } \left( k a / 2 \right) \approx 1 , \nu = { \frac { \varLambda } { 2 } } .$ (~ ) = tanh (时)但 M=;

所以 $E = - { \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } \nu ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } } } \approx - { \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } \varLambda ^ { 2 } } { 8 m a ^ { 2 } } } = - { \frac { m \lambda ^ { 2 } } { 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } } }$ 一这与 $\delta$ 函数势阱的能量一致.

(e) 对于 $p = 1 / 2$ 的情况 $\nu = \frac { 1 } { 2 } \left| A \right| \operatorname { t a n h } \left( \frac { \nu } { 2 } \right)$ 可以看出，这个方程要有解就需要等式两边的函数至少有 个交点.因此，为了使这个结论成 ，等式右边的斜率在原点处必须大于 ，即

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \nu} \frac {1}{2} | \varLambda | \tanh \left(\frac {\nu}{2}\right) \bigg | _ {0} > 1, \quad \frac {| \varLambda |}{4} > 1.
$$

7.11

牢牢牢习题 7.54 在某系统第 $n$ 个能量本征态下计算可观测量。 $\varOmega$ 的期望值，系统受到 $H ^ { \prime }$ 微扰:

$$
\langle \varOmega \rangle = \langle \psi_ {n} | \hat {\varOmega} | \psi_ {n} \rangle .
$$

$\psi _ { n }$ 用本征态进行微扰展开，即方程 (7.5)，①

$$
\langle \varOmega \rangle = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| \hat {\varOmega} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle + \lambda \left[ \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| \hat {\varOmega} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| \hat {\varOmega} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle \right] + \lambda^ {2} (\dots) + \dots .
$$

因此 $\langle \varOmega \rangle$ 的一阶修正为

$$
\left\langle \varOmega \right\rangle^ {1} = 2 \operatorname {R e} \left[ \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| \hat {\varOmega} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle \right],
$$

或者，用方程 (7.13)

$$
\langle \varOmega \rangle^ {1} = 2 \mathrm {R e} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} | \hat {\varOmega} | \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \rangle \langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} | H ^ {\prime} | \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \rangle}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}
$$

(假定无微扰的能量是非简井的，或者我们选取了"好"的基态).

(a) 如果 $\varOmega = H ^ { \prime }$ 微扰本身)，在该情况下，方程 (7.118) 告诉了我们什么? (小心地)解释为什么这与方程 (7.15) 相符合  
(b) 考虑带电量 $q$ 的粒子(可能是氢原子中的一个电子，或者一个与弹簧相连接的木髓球)，放置在一沿 $x$ 方向的弱电场 $E _ { \mathrm { e x t } }$ 中，因此，

$$
H ^ {\prime} = - q E _ {\mathrm {e x t}} x.
$$

电场将在"原子"中诱导一个偶极矩， $p _ { e } = q x$ . $p _ { e }$ 的期望值正比于施加电场，比例因子被称为极化率 $\alpha$ . 证明:

$$
\alpha = - 2 q ^ {2} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} | x | \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \rangle \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}.
$$

求一维谐振子基态极化率.与经典结果作对比.

假设质量为 $m$ 的粒子处于一维谐振子势场中，井有一个小的非谐微扰①

$$
H ^ {\prime} = - \frac {1}{6} \kappa x ^ {3}.
$$

求在第 $n$ 个能量本征态下的(一阶)修正 $\langle x \rangle$ 答案: $\left( n + \frac { 1 } { 2 } \right) \hbar \kappa / ( 2 m ^ { 2 } \omega ^ { 3 } ) .$

注释 随着温度的升高，高能量态被占据，粒子(平均)运动距离远离平衡位置;这就是为什么固体随着温度升高而膨胀.

解答 (a) $\left. \varOmega \right. ^ { 1 } = 2 \mathrm { R e } \left[ \left. \psi _ { n } ^ { 0 } \right| \hat { \varOmega } \left| \psi _ { n } ^ { 1 } \right. \right]$ 结合方程 (7

$$
\psi_ {n} ^ {1} = \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} \psi_ {m} ^ {0}.
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \varOmega \rangle^ {1} = 2 \operatorname {R e} \sum_ {m \neq n} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| \hat {\varOmega} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \frac {\left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} \\ = 2 \mathrm {R e} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| \left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} \\ = 2 \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| \left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中用到 $\langle \Omega \rangle = H ^ { \prime }$ 其结果必为实数.

由方程 (7.15)

$$
E _ {n} ^ {2} = \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| \left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}},
$$

所以 $\left. \varOmega \right. ^ { 1 } = 2 E _ { n } ^ { 2 }$

解释因 $\frac {  \psi _ { n }  H _ { 0 } + \lambda H ^ { \prime }  \psi _ { n }  } {  \psi _ { n }  \psi _ { n } \rangle } = \frac {  \psi _ { n }  H _ { 0 }  \psi _ { n }  } {  \psi _ { n }  \psi _ { n }  } + \lambda \frac {  \psi _ { n }  H ^ { \prime }  \psi _ { n }  } {  \psi _ { n }  \psi _ { n }  }$ ，且已知 $\left. \Omega \right. ^ { \prime } = \lambda \frac { \left. \psi _ { n } | H ^ { \prime } | \psi _ { n } \right. } { \left. \psi _ { n } | \psi _ { n } \right. } =$ $2 E _ { n } ^ { 2 }$ 所以，我们只需考虑右边第一项

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. \langle \psi_ {n} \mid H _ {0} \mid \psi_ {n} \rangle = \left\langle \left(\psi_ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda \psi_ {n} ^ {1} + \lambda^ {2} \psi_ {n} ^ {2} + \dots \right\rvert H _ {0} \mid \psi_ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda \psi_ {n} ^ {1} + \lambda^ {2} \psi_ {n} ^ {2} + \dots \right\rangle \right. \\ = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle + \lambda \left[ \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right] \\ + \lambda^ {2} \left[ \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {2} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {2} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle \right] + \dots \\ \approx E _ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda E _ {n} ^ {0} \left[ \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} | \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \rangle + \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} | \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \rangle \right] \\ + \lambda^ {2} \left[ E _ {n} ^ {0} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \mid \psi_ {n} ^ {2} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle + E _ {n} ^ {0} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle \right] \\ = E _ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda^ {2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} | H _ {0} | \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

由归一化条件

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \psi_ {n} | \psi_ {n} \rangle = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda \psi_ {n} ^ {1} + \lambda^ {2} \psi_ {n} ^ {2} + \dots | \psi_ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda \psi_ {n} ^ {1} + \lambda^ {2} \psi_ {n} ^ {2} + \dots \right\rangle \\ = \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} | \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \rangle + \lambda \left[ \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} | \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \rangle + \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} | \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \rangle \right] \\ + \lambda^ {2} \left[ \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} | \psi_ {n} ^ {2} \rangle + \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} | \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \rangle + \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {2} | \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \rangle \right] + \dots \\ \approx 1 + \lambda^ {2} \left<   \psi_ {n} ^ {1} | \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right > , \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\langle \psi_ {n} | H _ {0} | \psi_ {n} \rangle}{\langle \psi_ {n} | \psi_ {n} \rangle} = \frac {E _ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda^ {2} \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} | H _ {0} | \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \rangle}{1 + \lambda^ {2} \langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} | \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \rangle} \\ = \left[ E _ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda^ {2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle \right] \cdot \left[ 1 - \lambda^ {2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} | \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle \right] \\ = E _ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda^ {2} \left[ \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle - E _ {n} ^ {0} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \mid \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle \right] \\ = E _ {n} ^ {0} + \lambda^ {2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} - E _ {n} ^ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

先考虑 $\left. \psi _ { n } ^ { 1 } \right. H _ { 0 } - E _ { n } ^ { 0 } \left. \psi _ { n } ^ { 1 } \right.$ ~A--t..m ('7 1 ')\ ，结口方程 (7.13) $\psi _ { n } ^ { 1 } = \sum _ { m \neq n } { \frac { \left. \psi _ { m } ^ { 0 } \right. H ^ { \prime } \left. \psi _ { n } ^ { 0 } \right. } { E _ { n } ^ { 0 } - E _ { m } ^ { 0 } } } \psi _ { m } ^ { 0 }$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} - E _ {n} ^ {0} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} - E _ {n} ^ {0} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| H _ {0} - E _ {n} ^ {0} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {H _ {m n} ^ {\prime}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left(E _ {m} ^ {0} - E _ {n} ^ {0}\right) H _ {m n} ^ {\prime}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \\ = - \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| \sum_ {m \neq n} H _ {m n} ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

再将 $\left. \psi _ { n } ^ { 1 } \right.$ 用方程 (7.13) 的共辄替换，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right| \sum_ {m \neq n} H _ {m n} ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle = - \sum_ {p \neq n} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {H _ {p n} ^ {\prime *}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {p} ^ {0}} H _ {m n} ^ {\prime} \left\langle \psi_ {p} ^ {0} | \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \\ = - \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| H _ {m n} ^ {\prime} \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} = - E _ {n} ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，能量二阶修正为 $- E _ { n } ^ { 2 } + 2 E _ { n } ^ { 2 } = E _ { n } ^ { 2 }$ .

(b) 由题知可观测量 $\varOmega$ E~ 的一阶修正可表示为 $\left. \varOmega \right. ^ { 1 } = 2 \mathrm { R e } \left[ \left. \psi _ { n } ^ { 0 } \right| \hat { \varOmega } \left| \psi _ { n } ^ { 1 } \right. \right]$

令。 $\varOmega = p x , H ^ { \prime } = - q E _ { \mathrm { e x t } } x$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle p x \right\rangle^ {\prime} = 2 \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| p x \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {1} \right\rangle \\ = 2 \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| p x \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| - q E _ {\mathrm {e x t}} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \\ = - 2 q ^ {2} E _ {\mathrm {e x t}} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| x \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\alpha = - 2 q ^ {2} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| \left<   \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| x \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right>\right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}.
$$

求一维谐振子的极化率，要先求矩阵元 $\left. \psi _ { n } ^ { 0 } \right| x \left| \psi _ { m } ^ { 0 } \right.$ ，由升降阶算符

$$
a _ {\pm} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 m \omega \hbar}} (m \omega x \mp \mathrm {i} p),
$$

$$
a _ {+} \psi_ {n} = \sqrt {n + 1} \psi_ {n + 1},
$$

$$
a _ {-} \psi_ {n} = \sqrt {n} \psi_ {n - 1},
$$

得

$$
x = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(a _ {+} + a _ {-}\right).
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| x \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| a _ {+} + a _ {-} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left[ \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| a _ {+} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| a _ {-} \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left[ \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| \sqrt {m + 1} \left| \psi_ {m + 1} ^ {0} \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| \sqrt {m} \left| \psi_ {m - 1} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，

$$
\alpha = - 2 q ^ {2} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\sqrt {m + 1} \delta_ {n , m + 1} + \sqrt {m} \delta_ {n , m - 1}\right) ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}.
$$

基态情况下 $n = 0$

$$
\alpha = - 2 q ^ {2} \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \sum_ {m \neq 0} \frac {\left(\sqrt {m + 1} \delta_ {0 , m + 1} + \sqrt {m} \delta_ {0 , m - 1}\right) ^ {2}}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}}
$$

$$
= - 2 q ^ {2} \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \cdot \frac {1}{\frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega - \frac {3}{2} \hbar \omega} = \frac {q ^ {2}}{m \omega^ {2}}.
$$

经典情况下，电场力与弹性力相等，即 $q { \cal E } _ { \mathrm { e x t } } = k d$ .

由 $p _ { e } = q d = q { \frac { q E _ { \mathrm { e x t } } } { k } }$ 则 $\alpha = \frac { q ^ { 2 } } { k }$ ，再由 $\omega = { \sqrt { \frac { k } { m } } }$ ，所以 $\alpha = \frac { q ^ { 2 } } { m \omega ^ { 2 } }$ 与量子情况一致￡，

(c) 令。 $\varOmega = x$ ，则 $x$ 期望值的一阶微扰为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle^ {\prime} = 2 \mathrm {R e} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| x \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle}{E _ {n} ^ {0} - E _ {m} ^ {0}} \\ = 2 \mathrm {R e} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right| x \left| \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| - \frac {1}{6} \kappa x ^ {3} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle}{\left(n + \frac {1}{2}\right) \hbar \omega - \left(m + \frac {1}{2}\right) \hbar \omega} \\ = - \frac {k}{3 \hbar \omega} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \mathrm {R e} \left[ \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {(\sqrt {m} \delta_ {n , m - 1} + \sqrt {n} \delta_ {m , n - 1})}{n - m} \left\langle \psi_ {m} ^ {0} \right| x ^ {3} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right] \\ = - \frac {k}{3 \hbar \omega} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \mathrm {R e} \left[ - \sqrt {n + 1} \left\langle \psi_ {n + 1} ^ {0} \right| x ^ {3} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle + \sqrt {n} \left\langle \psi_ {n - 1} ^ {0} \right| x ^ {3} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

z= $x = \sqrt { \frac { \hbar } { 2 m \omega } } \left( a _ { + } + a _ { - } \right)$ ，得

$$
x ^ {3} = \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {2} \left(a _ {+} ^ {3} + a _ {+} ^ {2} a _ {-} + a _ {+} a _ {-} a _ {+} + a _ {+} a _ {-} ^ {2} + a _ {-} a _ {+} ^ {2} + a _ {-} a _ {+} a _ {-} + a _ {-} ^ {2} a _ {+} + a _ {-} ^ {3}\right),
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \psi_ {n + 1} ^ {0} \right| x ^ {3} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle = \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}} \left\langle n + 1 \right| \left(a _ {+} ^ {3} + a _ {+} ^ {2} a _ {-} + a _ {+} a _ {-} a _ {+} + a _ {+} a _ {-} ^ {2} + a _ {-} a _ {+} ^ {2} \right. \\ \left. + a _ {-} a _ {+} a _ {-} + a _ {-} ^ {2} a _ {+} + a _ {-} ^ {3}\right) | n \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

由正变性定理知，我们只需要得到 $x ^ { 3 } \left| \psi _ { n } ^ { 0 } \right. = \lambda \left| \psi _ { n + 1 } ^ { 0 } \right.$ 项即可， $\lambda$ 为待求系数.

因此 $a _ { + } ^ { 2 } a _ { - } + a _ { + } a _ { - } a _ { + } + a _ { - } a _ { + } ^ { 2 }$ 是有效项，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} a _ {+} ^ {2} a _ {-} | n \rangle = a _ {+} ^ {2} \sqrt {n} | n - 1 \rangle = a _ {+} \sqrt {n} \sqrt {n} | n \rangle \\ = \sqrt {n} \sqrt {n} \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle = n \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} a _ {+} a _ {-} a _ {+} | n \rangle = a _ {+} a _ {-} \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle = a _ {+} \sqrt {n + 1} \sqrt {n + 1} | n \rangle \\ = \sqrt {n + 1} \sqrt {n + 1} \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle = n + 1 \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} a _ {-} a _ {+} ^ {2} | n \rangle = a _ {-} a _ {+} \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle = a _ {-} \sqrt {n + 1} \sqrt {n + 2} | n + 2 \rangle \\ = \sqrt {n + 1} \sqrt {n + 2} \sqrt {n + 2} | n + 1 \rangle = (n + 2) \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

$\left. \psi _ { n - 1 } ^ { 0 } \right| x ^ { 3 } \left| \psi _ { n } ^ { 0 } \right.$ 的非零项要求 $x ^ { 3 } \left| \psi _ { n } ^ { 0 } \right. = \lambda ^ { \prime } \left| \psi _ { n - 1 } ^ { 0 } \right. , \lambda ^ { \prime }$ 为待求系数.

困此 $a _ { + } a _ { - } ^ { 2 } + a _ { - } a _ { + } a _ { - } + a _ { - } ^ { 2 } a _ { + }$ 是有效项，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} a _ {+} a _ {-} ^ {2} | n \rangle = a _ {+} a _ {-} \sqrt {n} | n - 1 \rangle = a _ {+} \sqrt {n} \sqrt {n - 1} | n - 2 \rangle \\ = \sqrt {n} \sqrt {n - 1} \sqrt {n - 1} | n - 1 \rangle = (n - 1) \sqrt {n} | n - 1 \rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} a _ {-} a _ {+} a _ {-} \left| n \right\rangle = a _ {-} a _ {+} \sqrt {n} \left| n - 1 \right\rangle = a _ {-} \sqrt {n} \sqrt {n} \left| n \right\rangle \\ = \sqrt {n} \sqrt {n} \sqrt {n} | n - 1 \rangle = n \sqrt {n} | n - 1 \rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} a _ {-} ^ {2} a _ {+} | n \rangle = a _ {-} ^ {2} \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle = a _ {-} \sqrt {n + 1} \sqrt {n + 1} | n \rangle \\ = \sqrt {n + 1} \sqrt {n + 1} \sqrt {n} | n - 1 \rangle = (n + 1) \sqrt {n} | n - 1 \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

故

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \psi_ {n + 1} ^ {0} \right| x ^ {3} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {0} \right\rangle = \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}} \left[ n \sqrt {n + 1} + (n + 1) \sqrt {n + 1} + (n + 2) \sqrt {n + 1} \right] \\ = \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}} \left[ (n - 1) \sqrt {n} + n \sqrt {n} + (n + 1) \sqrt {n} \right] \\ = \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}} \cdot 3 n \sqrt {n}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle^ {\prime} = - \frac {k}{3 \hbar \omega} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \mathrm {R e} \left[ - \sqrt {n + 1} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}} \cdot 3 (n + 1) \sqrt {n + 1} \right. \\ \left. + \sqrt {n} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {\frac {3}{2}} \cdot 3 n \sqrt {n} \right] \\ = (2 n + 1) \frac {\hbar k}{4 m ^ {2} \omega^ {3}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

习题 7.55 克兰德尔之谜.①一维定态薛定诗方程通常遵循"三个基本经验法则":(1) 能量非筒井; (2) 基态无节点，第一激发态有一个节点，第二激发态有两个节点，等等; 如果势场是 $x$ 的偶函数，基态是偶函数，第一激发态是奇函数，第二激发态是偶函数，等等.我们已经看到"环上的一个珠子" (习题 .46) 违反了第一条规则;现在假设在原点处引入了一个"刻痕飞

$$
H ^ {\prime} = - \alpha \delta (x).
$$

(如果你不喜欢 $\delta$ 函数，可以像习题 7.9 中那样变成高斯函数.)这消除了简井，但是偶波函数和奇波函数的次序是什么?节点数的次序是什么?提示 你不需要做任何计算，这里可以认为 $\alpha$ 足够小，但是如果你愿意，可以精确求解薛定谤方程.

解答由习题 .4

$$
\psi_ {n} ^ {+} (x) = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}}, \quad \psi_ {n} ^ {-} (x) = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}}, \quad E _ {n} = \frac {2 n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m L ^ {2}};
$$

对于基 $\psi _ { 0 } \left( x \right) = \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { L } }$ $E _ { 0 } = 0$ 对于非基 $n \neq 0$ ，则能量是三重筒并的

基态能量的一阶修正为

$$
E _ {0} ^ {1} = \int_ {0} ^ {L} \psi_ {0} ^ {*} (x) [ - \alpha \delta (x) ] \psi_ {0} (x) d L
$$

$$
= \int_ {0} ^ {L} \frac {1}{\sqrt {L}} [ - \alpha \delta (x) ] \frac {1}{\sqrt {L}} d L = - \frac {\alpha}{L}.
$$

对于其他简并情况，构建 矩阵:

令

$$
\psi_ {n} ^ {a} = \psi_ {n} ^ {+} (x) = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}}, \quad \psi_ {n} ^ {b} = \psi_ {n} ^ {-} (x) = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}};
$$

则

$$
W _ {a a} = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {a} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {a} \right\rangle = \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}} [ - \alpha \delta (x) ] \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}} \mathrm {d} x = - \frac {\alpha}{L};
$$

同理 h=-2 $W _ { b b } = - \frac { \alpha } { L }$

$$
W _ {a b} = \left\langle \psi_ {n} ^ {a} \right| H ^ {\prime} \left| \psi_ {n} ^ {b} \right\rangle = \int \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}} [ - \alpha \delta (x) ] \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}} \mathrm {d} x = - \frac {\alpha}{L};
$$

同理 $W _ { b a } = - \frac { \alpha } { L }$

因此，

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {W}} = - \frac {\alpha}{L} \left[ \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ 1 & 1 \end{array} \right];
$$

解久期方程 $- { \frac { \alpha } { L } } \left| \begin{array} { c c } { { 1 - \lambda } } & { { 1 } } \\ { { 1 } } & { { 1 - \lambda } } \end{array} \right| = 0$ ，得 $\lambda = 0$ 或 $\lambda = 2$

$\lambda = 0$ 时， $E _ { + } ^ { \mathrm { ~ ~ } } = - \frac { \alpha } { L } \cdot 0 = 0$

$$
\psi_ {n} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {n} ^ {a} (x) + \psi_ {n} ^ {b} (x) \right] = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 L}} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}\right) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{L}} \mathrm {i} \sin (2 n \pi x / L).
$$

$\lambda = 2$ 时， $E _ { - } = - \frac { 2 \alpha } { L }$

$$
\psi_ {n} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {n} ^ {a} (x) + \psi_ {n} ^ {b} (x) \right] = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 L}} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L} + \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}\right) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{L}} \cos (2 n \pi x / L).
$$

此时，我们可以得到 $n$ 对应的能量、节点数和宇称，如表 7.4.

.4   

<table><tr><td>n</td><td>E</td><td>节点数</td><td>字称</td><td>波函数</td></tr><tr><td>0</td><td>-α/L</td><td>0</td><td>偶</td><td>1/√L</td></tr><tr><td>1</td><td>-2α/L + 2n2h2/L2m</td><td>2</td><td>偶</td><td>√2/L cos(2πx/L)</td></tr><tr><td>1</td><td>2n2h2/L2m</td><td>2</td><td>奇</td><td>√2/L sin(2πx/L)</td></tr><tr><td>2</td><td>-2α/L + 8n2h2/L2m</td><td>4</td><td>偶</td><td>√2/L cos(4πx/L)</td></tr><tr><td>2</td><td>8n2h2/L2m</td><td>4</td><td>奇</td><td>√2/L sin(4πx/L)</td></tr><tr><td>n</td><td>E</td><td>节点数</td><td>宇称</td><td>波函数</td></tr><tr><td>3</td><td>-2α/L + 18n2h2/L2m</td><td>6</td><td>偶</td><td>√2/L cos(6πx/L)</td></tr><tr><td>3</td><td>18n2h2/L2m</td><td>6</td><td>偶</td><td>√2/L sin(6πx/L)</td></tr></table>

事**习题 7.56 在本题中，将电子-电子的排斥项作为氯原子哈密顿量(方程 (5.38))中的微扰，

$$
H ^ {\prime} = \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {e ^ {2}}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} |}.
$$

(这是不精确的，因为微扰对比于电子与核之间的库仑作用而言并不是一个小量..... .但这是一个开始.)

(a) 求基态的一阶修正

$$
\psi_ {0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) = \psi_ {1 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {1 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right).
$$

(如果你做了习题 5.15，你已经完成了此计算，不过在那时我们并没有称之为微扰理论.)

(b) 现在处理第一激发态，一个电子处于类氢原子的基态 $\psi _ { 1 0 0 }$ ，另一个处于态$\psi _ { 2 0 0 }$ ，事实上，存在两个这样的态，它依赖于电子自旋是单态(仲氯)还是三重态(正氨) :①

$$
\psi_ {\pm} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {1 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {2 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \pm \psi_ {2 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {1 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \right].
$$

证明:

$$
E _ {\pm} ^ {1} = \frac {1}{2} (K \pm J),
$$

其中

$$
K \equiv 2 \int \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) H ^ {\prime} \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2},
$$

$$
J \equiv 2 \int \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) H ^ {\prime} \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}.
$$

估计这两个积分，输入具体的数值，将结果与教材图 进行对比(测量值为 $- 5 9 . 2 \ : \mathrm { e V }$ 和 $- 5 8 . 4 \ \mathrm { e V }$ .~

解答 (a) 基态的一阶微扰为

$$
E _ {0} ^ {1} = \langle \psi_ {0} | \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} | \psi_ {0} \rangle = \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left\langle \frac {1}{r} \right\rangle ,
$$

其中 ${ \frac { 1 } { r } } = { \frac { 1 } { \left| r _ { 1 } - r _ { 2 } \right| } }$ ' ，由习题 _..- 5.15(a) _._-,-, 知 $\left. { \frac { 1 } { r } } \right. = { \frac { 5 } { 4 a } }$ ，所以

$$
E _ {0} ^ {1} = \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \cdot \frac {5}{4 a} = \frac {5 e ^ {2}}{1 6 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a} = \frac {5}{2} E _ {1} = \frac {5}{2} \times 1 3. 6 \mathrm {e V} = 3 4 \mathrm {e V}.
$$

(b) 对于第一激发态，一个电子处于态 $\psi _ { 1 0 0 }$ ，另一个电子处于态 $\psi _ { 2 0 0 }$ 时，披函数为

$$
\psi_ {\pm} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {1 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {2 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \pm \psi_ {2 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {1 0 0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \right].
$$

由子晗密顿量在 $\mathbf { \Delta } _ { \mathbf { \mathscr { r } } _ { 1 } }$ 和 $\mathbf { \boldsymbol { r } } _ { 2 }$ 的交换作用下保持不变，这两个态在交换作用下保持奇和偶字称，所以微扰不会使这两个态发生交叠，即 $w$ 矩阵的非对角项为零.

因此，可以按照非简并情况处理

$$
\begin{array}{l} E _ {\pm} ^ {1} = \left\langle \psi_ {\pm} \right| \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} | \psi_ {\pm} \rangle = \frac {1}{2} \iint \left[ \psi_ {1 0 0} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {2 0 0} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \right. \\ \pm \psi_ {1 0 0} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {2 0 0} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \\ \pm \psi_ {2 0 0} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {1 0 0} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \\ \left. \pm \psi_ {2 0 0} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {1 0 0} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \right] d ^ {3} r _ {1} d ^ {3} r _ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

将上式第三项和第四项的 $\mathbf { \Delta } _ { \mathbf { r } _ { 1 } }$ 和 $\mathbf { \boldsymbol { r } } _ { 2 }$ 交换，可以得到第三项与第二项等价，第四项与第一项等价.困此，上式可写为 $E _ { \pm } ^ { 1 } = 2 \left( K \pm J \right)$ 其中

$$
K = 2 \int | \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) | ^ {2} \int | \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) | ^ {2} \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r _ {1} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r _ {2},
$$

$$
J = 2 \int \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \int \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r _ {1} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r _ {2}.
$$

$K$ 中 $V _ { 1 } = \int { \left| \psi _ { 2 0 0 } \left( \pmb { r } _ { 1 } \right) \right| ^ { 2 } } \frac { e ^ { 2 } } { 4 \pi \varepsilon _ { 0 } r } \mathrm { d } ^ { 3 } r _ { 1 }$ 川

$$
\begin{array}{l} V _ {1} = \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \int \frac {8}{\pi a ^ {3}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 r _ {1} / a} \frac {a}{| \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} - \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} |} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \\ = 2 \left| E _ {1} \right| \frac {1 6}{a ^ {3}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 r _ {1} / a} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {a \sin \theta_ {1} r _ {1} ^ {2}}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} \mathrm {d} r _ {1}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {a \sin \theta_ {1}}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} d \theta_ {1} = \int_ {- 1} ^ {1} \frac {a}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} u}} d u (u = \cos \theta_ {1}) \\ = \frac {a}{r _ {1} r _ {2}} \left(r _ {1} + r _ {2} - | r _ {1} - r _ {2} |\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 2 a / r _ {1}, & r _ {1} > r _ {2} \\ 2 a / r _ {2}, & r _ {1} <   r _ {2} \end{array} \right., \\ \end{array}
$$

将上式代入 $V _ { 1 }$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} V _ {1} = 2 \left| E _ {1} \right| \frac {3 2}{a ^ {3}} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {r _ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 r _ {1} / a} \frac {a}{r _ {2}} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} + \int_ {r _ {2}} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 r _ {1} / a} a r _ {1} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \right] \\ = | E _ {1} | \left\{\frac {a}{r _ {2}} \left[ 2 - \left(2 + \frac {8 r ^ {2}}{a} + \frac {1 6 r _ {2} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 r _ {2} / a} \right] + 4 \left(1 + \frac {4 r _ {2}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 r _ {2} / a} \right\} \\ = 2 \left| E _ {1} \right| \left[ \frac {a}{r _ {2}} - \left(2 + \frac {a}{r _ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 r _ {2} / a} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

由于 $V _ { 1 }$ 和 $\psi _ { 2 0 0 }$ 只依赖 $\mathbf { \boldsymbol { r } } _ { 2 }$ ，所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} K = 8 \pi \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} | \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) | ^ {2} V _ {1} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) r _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \\ = 8 \pi \cdot 2 | E _ {1} | \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{\pi a ^ {3}} \left(1 - \frac {r _ {2}}{a}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r _ {2} / a} \left[ \frac {r _ {2}}{a} - \left(2 + \frac {a}{r _ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 4 r _ {2} / a} \right] r _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \\ = \frac {1 6}{a ^ {3}} \left| E _ {1} \right| \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} a r _ {2} \left(1 - \frac {r _ {2}}{a}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 r _ {2} / a} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \right. \\ \left. - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(2 r _ {2} ^ {2} + a r _ {2}\right) \left(1 - \frac {r _ {2}}{a}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 6 r _ {2} / a} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

令 $Z = \frac { 2 r _ { 2 } } { a }$ 2r2 和 $z = \frac { 6 r _ { 2 } } { a }$ 6r2 ，在第一个积分和第二个积分中作代换，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} K = \frac {1 6}{a ^ {3}} | E _ {1} | \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {a ^ {2} Z}{2} \left(1 - \frac {Z}{2}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- Z} \frac {a}{2} \mathrm {d} Z \right. \\ \left. - \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(\frac {1}{1 8} a ^ {2} z ^ {2} + \frac {1}{6} a ^ {2} z\right) \left(1 - \frac {z}{6}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- z} \frac {a}{6} \mathrm {d} z \right] \\ = 2 \left| E _ {1} \right| \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1 6}{9} z - 2 z ^ {2} + \frac {1 4}{2 7} z ^ {3} - \frac {1}{4 8 6} z ^ {4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- Z} \mathrm {d} z \\ = \frac {1 3 6}{8 1} | E _ {1} | = 2 2. 8 3 \mathrm {e V}. \\ \end{array}
$$

对于 $J$

$$
J = 2 \int \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \int \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r _ {1} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r _ {2}.
$$

令

$$
\begin{array}{l} V _ {2} = \int \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} r _ {1} \\ = \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int \left(1 - \frac {r _ {1}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {1} / a} \frac {a}{\left| r _ {1} - r _ {2} \right|} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \\ = \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \mathrm {d} \varphi \int \left(1 - \frac {r _ {1}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {1} / a} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {a \sin \theta_ {1}}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 r _ {1} r _ {2} \cos \theta_ {1}}} \mathrm {d} \theta_ {1} r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \\ = 2 \left| E _ {1} \right| \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{\pi a ^ {3}} 2 \pi \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {r _ {1}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {1} / a} z _ {1} (r _ {1}) r _ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {1}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $z _ { 1 }$ 与 $K$ 中计算用到的 $z _ { 1 }$ 一样，因此通过分段积分可得

$$
V _ {2} = | E _ {1} | \frac {1 6 \sqrt {2}}{a ^ {3}} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {r _ {2}} \frac {a r _ {1} ^ {2}}{r _ {2}} \left(1 - \frac {r _ {1}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {1} / a} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} + \int_ {r _ {2}} ^ {\infty} a r _ {1} \left(1 - \frac {r _ {1}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {1} / a} \mathrm {d} r _ {1} \right].
$$

令 $Z = { \frac { r _ { 1 } } { a } }$ ， 得

$$
\begin{array}{l} V _ {2} = \left| E _ {1} \right| \frac {1 6 \sqrt {2}}{a ^ {3}} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {3 r _ {2} / a} \frac {a ^ {3} z ^ {2}}{a r _ {2}} \left(1 - \frac {z}{3}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- z} \frac {a}{3} \mathrm {d} z + \int_ {3 r _ {2} / a} ^ {\infty} \frac {a ^ {2} z}{3} \left(1 - \frac {z}{3}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- z} \frac {a}{3} \mathrm {d} z \right] \\ = \left| E _ {1} \right| \frac {1 6 \sqrt {2}}{9} \left[ \frac {a}{3 r _ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {3 r _ {2} / a} \left(z ^ {2} - \frac {z ^ {3}}{3}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- z} \frac {a}{3} \mathrm {d} z + \int_ {3 r _ {2} / a} ^ {\infty} \left(z - \frac {z ^ {2}}{3}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- z} \frac {a}{3} \mathrm {d} z \right] \\ = \left| E _ {1} \right| \frac {1 6 \sqrt {2}}{9} \left\{\frac {a}{3 r _ {2}} \left\{2 - \left(2 + \frac {6 r _ {2}}{a} + \frac {9 r _ {2} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {2} / a} \right. \right. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left. - \left[ 2 - \left(2 + \frac {6 r _ {2}}{a} + \frac {9 r _ {2} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {9 r _ {2} ^ {3}}{a ^ {3}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {2} / a} \right] \right\} \\ \left. + \left(1 + \frac {3 r _ {1}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {2} / a} - \frac {1}{3} \left(2 + \frac {6 r _ {2}}{a} + \frac {9 r _ {2} ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {2} / a} \right\} \\ = \left| E _ {1} \right| \frac {1 6 \sqrt {2}}{2 7} \left(1 + \frac {3 r _ {1}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {2} / a}. \\ \end{array}
$$

将 $V _ { 2 }$ 代入 $J$ 的表达式中，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} J = 8 \pi \int \psi_ {2 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \psi_ {1 0 0} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) V _ {2} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) r _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \\ = 8 \pi | E _ {1} | \frac {1 6 \sqrt {2}}{2 7} \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{\pi a ^ {3}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {r _ {2}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {2} / a} \left(1 + \frac {3 r _ {2}}{a}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 r _ {2} / a} r _ {2} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} r _ {2} \\ = \frac {5 1 8}{2 7} | E _ {1} | \frac {1}{6} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {z}{6}\right) \left(1 + \frac {z}{2}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- z} z ^ {2} \mathrm {d} z \\ = \frac {6 4}{7 2 9} \left| E _ {1} \right| \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(z ^ {2} + \frac {z ^ {3}}{3} - \frac {z ^ {4}}{1 2}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- z} \mathrm {d} z \\ = \frac {1 2 8}{7 2 9} \left| E _ {1} \right| = 2. 3 9 \mathrm {e V}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，总能为

$$
E _ {0} + \frac {1}{2} (K + J) = 4 E _ {1} + 4 \times \frac {E _ {1}}{4} - \frac {6 8}{8 1} E _ {1} - \frac {6 4}{7 2 9} E _ {1} = - 5 5. 3 9 \mathrm {e V},
$$

$$
E _ {0} + \frac {1}{2} (K - J) = 4 E _ {1} + 4 \times \frac {E _ {1}}{4} - \frac {6 8}{8 1} E _ {1} + \frac {6 4}{7 2 9} E _ {1} = - 5 7. 7 8 \mathrm {e V}.
$$

习题 通过定义 $H ^ { 0 }$ 和 $H ^ { \prime }$ 布洛赫函数(方程 )的哈密顿量可以用微扰理论来分析，

$$
H ^ {0} u _ {n 0} = E _ {n 0} u _ {n 0},
$$

$$
\left(H ^ {0} + H ^ {\prime}\right) u _ {n q} = E _ {n q} u _ {n q}.
$$

在本题中，不要假设任何形式的 $V \left( x \right)$ .

(a) 确定算符 $H ^ { 0 }$ 和 $H ^ { \prime }$ （用 $\hat { p }$ 项表示).  
(b) $E _ { n q }$ 精确到 $q$ 的二阶，即求 $A _ { n } , B _ { n }$ 和 $C _ { n }$ 的表达式(用 $E _ { n 0 }$ 和 $\hat { p }$ 在无微扰态 $u _ { n 0 }$ 中的矩阵元表示)

$$
E _ {n q} \approx A _ {n} + B _ {n} q + C _ {n} q ^ {2}.
$$

(c) 证明:所有的常数 $B _ { n }$ 都为零.提示见习题 2.1(b). 记住 $u _ { n 0 } \left( x \right)$ 是周期性的

注释 习惯上写 $C _ { n } = \hbar ^ { 2 } / ( 2 m _ { n } ^ { * } )$ ，其中 $\boldsymbol { m } _ { n } ^ { * }$ 是粒子在第 $n$ 个带中的有效质量，正如刚才所证明的那样

$$
E _ {n q} \approx \mathrm {c o n s t a n t} + \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m _ {n} ^ {*}},
$$

当 $k  q$ 时，它像自由粒子一样(方程 (2.92)).

解答 (a) 布洛赫波函数可以写为周期性调制的平面波的形式(方程 (6.12))

$$
\psi (x) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} q x} u (x).
$$

由方程 (6.14) 知，

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} u _ {n q}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} - \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar^ {2} q}{m} \frac {\mathrm {d} u _ {n q}}{\mathrm {d} x} + V (x) u _ {n q} = \left(E _ {n q} - \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m}\right) u _ {n q}.
$$

当 $q = 0$ 时，上式变为

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} u _ {n 0}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + V (x) u _ {n 0} = E _ {n 0} u _ {n 0},
$$

即 $H _ { 0 } = - \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m } \frac { \mathrm { d } ^ { 2 } } { \mathrm { d } ^ { 2 } x } + V \left( x \right) = \frac { p ^ { 2 } } { 2 m } + V \left( x \right) .$ 2m

当 $q \neq 0$ 时，

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} u _ {n q}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} - \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar^ {2} q}{m} \frac {\mathrm {d} u _ {n q}}{\mathrm {d} x} + V (x) u _ {n q} = \left(E _ {n q} - \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m}\right) u _ {n q}, \\ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} u _ {n q} + \frac {\hbar q p}{m} u _ {n q} + V (x) u _ {n q} = \left(E _ {n q} - \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m}\right) u _ {n q}, \\ \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + V (x) + \left(\frac {\hbar q p}{m} + \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m}\right) \right] u _ {n q} = E _ {n q} u _ {n q}, \\ \end{array}
$$

因此 $H ^ { \prime } = \frac { \hbar q p } { m } + \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } q ^ { 2 } } { 2 m }$ hqp ~m

(b) 由一阶微扰和二阶微扰理论可知，

$$
E _ {n q} = E _ {n 0} + \langle u _ {n 0} | H ^ {\prime} | u _ {n 0} \rangle + \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {| \langle u _ {m 0} | H ^ {\prime} | u _ {n 0} \rangle | ^ {2}}{E _ {n 0} - E _ {m 0}},
$$

由于

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle u _ {m 0} | H ^ {\prime} | u _ {n 0} \rangle = \langle u _ {m 0} | \frac {\hbar q p}{m} + \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m} | u _ {n 0} \rangle \\ = \frac {\hbar q}{m} \left\langle u _ {m 0} \right| p \left| u _ {n 0} \right\rangle + \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m} \left\langle u _ {m 0} \right| u _ {n 0} \rangle \\ = \frac {\hbar q}{m} \left\langle u _ {m 0} \right| p \left| u _ {n 0} \right\rangle + \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m} \delta_ {m n}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
E _ {n q} = E _ {n 0} + \frac {\hbar q}{m} \left\langle u _ {n 0} \right| p \left| u _ {n 0} \right\rangle + \frac {\hbar^ {2} q ^ {2}}{2 m} + \left(\frac {\hbar q}{m}\right) ^ {2} \sum_ {m \neq n} \frac {\left| \left\langle u _ {m 0} \right| p \left| u _ {n 0} \right\rangle \right| ^ {2}}{E _ {n 0} - E _ {m 0}}.
$$

(c) 由习题 2.1(b) 可知 $u _ { n 0 }$ 可以选为实数.因此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle u _ {n 0} \right| p \left| u _ {n 0} \right\rangle = \int_ {0} ^ {a} u _ {n 0} (x) \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) u _ {n 0} (x) \mathrm {d} x \\ = - \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} [ u _ {n 0} (x) ] ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x \\ = - \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \mathrm {d} [ u _ {n 0} (x) ] ^ {2} \\ = - \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{2} \left[ u _ {n 0} (x) \right] ^ {2} \big | _ {0} ^ {a} \\ = - \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{2} \left(\left[ u _ {n 0} (a) \right] ^ {2} - \left[ u _ {n 0} (0) \right] ^ {2}\right) \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$
